      }
      const recovered = await this.readback(operationId, actorId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId).catch(() => null);
      if (this.isExactPrepared(recovered, operationId, ownerId, actorId, authorized.draft, requestHash, resolved.context, definition)) {
        try { return await this.projectOperation(operationId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId); }
        catch {
          throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE',
            operationId, phase: 'PREPARED', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED',
            projectionPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
        }
      }
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
    }

    try {
      await this.preflightName(resolved.provider, resolved.context, definition);
    } catch (error) {
      const code = error instanceof ConflictException ? 'TEMPLATE_NAME_UNAVAILABLE' : 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE';
      await this.finishRejected(operationId, ownerId, undefined, 'REJECTED', code, false, actorId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId);
      if (error instanceof ConflictException) throw error;
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code, phase: 'REJECTED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
    }

    const dispatchClaimId = randomUUID();
    try {
      await this.transactions.run('claim', async (tx) => {
        await this.assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx, actorId, authorized.draft);
        const claimNow = await this.reserveAccountDispatch(tx, resolved.context.accountReference, operationId);
        const changed = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.updateMany({
          where: { id: operationId, buId: authorized.draft.buId, actorUserId: actorId,
            phase: 'PREPARED', version: 1, leaseOwnerId: ownerId, leaseUntil: { gt: claimNow } },
          data: { phase: 'SENDING', version: { increment: 1 }, dispatchClaimId,
            leaseUntil: new Date(claimNow.getTime() + LEASE_MS) },
        });
        if (changed.count !== 1) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_SUPERSEDED' });
        await tx.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.create({ data: {
          operationId, sequence: 2, phase: 'SENDING', safeCode: 'DISPATCH_CLAIMED',
        } });
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.statusOf(error) === 429) {
        await this.finishRejected(operationId, ownerId, undefined, 'REJECTED', 'TEMPLATE_RATE_LIMITED', false, actorId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId);
        throw error;
      }
      if (error instanceof ConflictException) throw error;
      const readback = await this.readback(operationId, actorId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId).catch(() => null);
      const claimConfirmed = Boolean(readback?.operation?.phase === 'SENDING'
        && readback.operation.dispatchClaimId === dispatchClaimId
        && readback.operation.leaseOwnerId === ownerId
        && readback.operation.leaseUntil && readback.operation.leaseUntil.getTime() > Date.now()
        && readback.operation.revision === input.expectedRevision
        && readback.reservation?.operationId === operationId
        && readback.operation.requestHash === requestHash
        && readback.operation.channelId === resolved.context.channelId
        && readback.operation.providerResourceIdentityId === resolved.context.providerResourceIdentityId
        && readback.operation.accountReference === resolved.context.accountReference
        && readback.operation.channelGeneration === resolved.context.generation
        && readback.operation.pairingEpoch === resolved.context.pairingEpoch
        && readback.operation.activeCredentialVersion === resolved.context.activeCredentialVersion
        && readback.operation.receipts[0]?.phase === 'SENDING'
        && readback.operation.receipts[0]?.safeCode === 'DISPATCH_CLAIMED'
        && readback.draft?.state === 'LOCKED' && readback.draft.revision === input.expectedRevision);
      if (!claimConfirmed) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
    }

    const dispatchGuard = {
      beforeDispatch: async () => {
        await revalidateAuth?.();
        const current = await this.authorizeDraft(actorId, authorized.draft.id, true);
        if (current.draft.revision !== input.expectedRevision || current.draft.state !== 'LOCKED') {
          throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_SUPERSEDED' });
        }
        await this.transactions.run('claim', async (tx) => {
          await this.assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx, actorId, authorized.draft);
          const actual = await this.resolver.resolve(reference, { transaction: tx });
          this.assertSameContext(resolved.context, actual.context);
          const [{ now }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
          const ownClaim = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
            id: operationId, actorUserId: actorId, buId: authorized.draft.buId,
            phase: 'SENDING', dispatchClaimId, leaseOwnerId: ownerId, leaseUntil: { gt: now },
            revision: input.expectedRevision, channelId: resolved.context.channelId,
            providerResourceIdentityId: resolved.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
            accountReference: resolved.context.accountReference, channelGeneration: resolved.context.generation,
            pairingEpoch: resolved.context.pairingEpoch, activeCredentialVersion: resolved.context.activeCredentialVersion,
          }, select: { id: true } });
          const [reservation, lockedDraft] = await Promise.all([
            tx.messageTemplateNameReservation.findUnique({ where: { operationId }, select: { operationId: true } }),
            tx.messageTemplateDraft.findFirst({ where: { id: authorized.draft.id, buId: authorized.draft.buId,
              mantenedoraId: authorized.draft.mantenedoraId, revision: input.expectedRevision, state: 'LOCKED' }, select: { id: true } }),
          ]);
          if (!ownClaim || !reservation || !lockedDraft) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_SUPERSEDED' });
        });
      },
    };

    let outcome: TemplateCreateOutcome;
    try {
      outcome = await resolved.provider.createTemplate({ context: resolved.context, definition, dispatchGuard });
    } catch {
      // Once the adapter call starts, an untyped throw cannot prove whether POST was sent.
      try { await this.recordOutcome(operationId, ownerId, dispatchClaimId, 'AMBIGUOUS', 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN'); }
      catch { throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', operationId, receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' }); }
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_UNCERTAIN', phase: 'AMBIGUOUS',
        outcome: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN', operationId, receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'REJECTED') {
      const code = outcome.dispatched ? 'PROVIDER_REFUSED' : 'PRE_DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE';
      return this.finishRejected(operationId, ownerId, dispatchClaimId, 'REJECTED', code, outcome.dispatched, actorId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId);
    }
    if (outcome.kind === 'AMBIGUOUS') {
      try { await this.recordOutcome(operationId, ownerId, dispatchClaimId, 'AMBIGUOUS', 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN'); }
      catch { throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', operationId, receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' }); }
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_UNCERTAIN', phase: 'AMBIGUOUS',
        outcome: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN', operationId, receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
    }
    try {
      await this.recordAcknowledgedOutcome(operationId, ownerId, dispatchClaimId, actorId, authorized.draft,
        requestHash, resolved.context, outcome.remoteTemplateId);
    } catch {
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE',
        outcome: 'ACCEPTED', phase: 'ACCEPTED', operationId, providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
        message: 'A Meta aceitou o cadastro, mas a conclusão local não foi confirmada; consulte esta operação antes de continuar' });
    }
    try {
      return await this.projectOperation(operationId, authorized.draft.buId, authorized.draft.mantenedoraId);
    } catch {
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', outcome: 'ACCEPTED',
        phase: 'ACCEPTED', operationId, providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED',
        projectionPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
        message: 'A Meta aceitou o cadastro, mas a conclusão local não foi confirmada; consulte esta operação antes de continuar' });
    }
  }

  async getOperation(actorId: string, draftId: string, operationId: string) {
    const { draft } = await this.authorizeDraft(actorId, draftId, true);
    const operation = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({
      where: { id: operationId, draftId, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId },
      select: { id: true },
    });
    if (!operation) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_OPERATION_NOT_FOUND' });
    return this.projectOperation(operation.id, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId);
  }

  async reconcile(actorId: string, draftId: string, operationId: string, revalidateAuth?: TemplateAuthRevalidation) {
    const { draft } = await this.authorizeDraft(actorId, draftId, true);
    const operation = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
      id: operationId, draftId, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
    } });
    if (!operation) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_OPERATION_NOT_FOUND' });
    if (operation.phase === 'ACCEPTED' || operation.phase === 'REJECTED') return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId);
    if (operation.phase === 'AMBIGUOUS') {
      if (operation.recoveryLeaseUntil && operation.recoveryLeaseUntil.getTime() > Date.now()) return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId).then((result) => ({ ...result, recoveryInProgress: true, retryAfter: 10 }));
      const reference: TemplateReference = { buId: operation.buId, channelId: operation.channelId, expectedGeneration: operation.channelGeneration };
      const resolved = await this.resolver.resolve(reference);
      this.assertBinding(draft, resolved.context);
      this.assertSameContext({ mantenedoraId: operation.mantenedoraId, buId: operation.buId, channelId: operation.channelId,
        providerResourceIdentityId: operation.providerResourceIdentityId, generation: operation.channelGeneration,
        pairingEpoch: operation.pairingEpoch, activeCredentialVersion: operation.activeCredentialVersion,
        accountReference: operation.accountReference as TemplateManagementContext['accountReference'] }, resolved.context);
      const recoveryOwnerId = randomUUID();
      if (!await this.claimReconciliation(actorId, draft, operation, recoveryOwnerId)) return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId).then((result) => ({ ...result, recoveryInProgress: true, retryAfter: 10 }));
      const definition = normalizeTemplateDefinition(operation.definitionSnapshot);
      try {
        const match = await this.findUniqueRemoteMatch(resolved.provider, resolved.context, definition);
        if (match) {
          await revalidateAuth?.();
          await this.recordCorrelatedAcceptance(actorId, draft, operation, resolved.context, match, recoveryOwnerId);
        }
        else await this.releaseReconciliationLease(operation, draft, recoveryOwnerId);
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ConflictException) throw error;
        if (error instanceof ServiceUnavailableException
          && (error.getResponse() as { code?: string }).code === 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE') throw error;
        await this.releaseReconciliationLease(operation, draft, recoveryOwnerId);
        throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RECONCILIATION_UNAVAILABLE' });
      }
      return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId);
    }
    try {
      const changed = await this.transactions.run('record-outcome', async (tx) => {
        await this.assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx, actorId, draft);
        const [{ now }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
        const target = operation.phase === 'PREPARED' ? 'REJECTED' : 'AMBIGUOUS';
        const updated = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.updateMany({
          where: { id: operationId, draftId, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
            actorUserId: operation.actorUserId, phase: operation.phase, leaseUntil: { lte: now } },
          data: { phase: target, safeCode: target === 'REJECTED' ? 'DISPATCH_NOT_STARTED' : 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN',
            leaseUntil: null, version: { increment: 1 } },
        });
        if (updated.count !== 1) return false;
        if (target === 'REJECTED') {
          await tx.messageTemplateNameReservation.deleteMany({ where: { operationId } });
          await tx.messageTemplateDraft.updateMany({ where: { id: draftId, buId: draft.buId,
            mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId, revision: (await tx.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId }, select: { revision: true } })).revision,
            state: 'LOCKED' }, data: { state: 'DRAFT' } });
        }
        const last = await tx.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.aggregate({ where: { operationId }, _max: { sequence: true } });
        await tx.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.create({ data: {
          operationId, sequence: (last._max.sequence ?? 0) + 1, phase: target,
          safeCode: target === 'REJECTED' ? 'DISPATCH_NOT_STARTED' : 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN',
        } });
        await this.registrarAtividade(tx, actorId, draft.mantenedoraId, draft.buId, operationId, 'template.operacao.reconciliada');
        return true;
      });
      if (!changed) return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId).then((result) => ({ ...result, recoveryInProgress: true, retryAfter: 10 }));
    } catch {
      const current = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
        id: operationId, draftId, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
        actorUserId: operation.actorUserId,
      }, select: { phase: true } }).catch(() => null);
      if (!current || !['REJECTED', 'AMBIGUOUS'].includes(current.phase)) {
        throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
      }
    }
    return this.projectOperation(operationId, draft.buId, draft.mantenedoraId);
  }

  private async findUniqueRemoteMatch(provider: MessageTemplateProvider, context: TemplateManagementContext, definition: TemplateDefinition): Promise<TemplateSummary | null> {
    const expected = templateDefinitionSemanticFingerprint(definition);
    let after: string | null = null;
    let found: TemplateSummary | null = null;
    for (let pageNumber = 0; pageNumber < 2; pageNumber += 1) {
      const page = await provider.listTemplates({ context, name: definition.name, after, limit: 50 });
      for (const summary of page.templates) {
        if (summary.name !== definition.name) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RECONCILIATION_INCONCLUSIVE' });
        if (summary.language === definition.language && summary.supported && summary.semanticFingerprint === expected) {
          if (found) return null;
          found = summary;
        }
      }
      if (!page.nextCursor) return found;
      after = page.nextCursor;
    }
    return null;
  }

  private async recordCorrelatedAcceptance(actorId: string, draft: MessageTemplateDraft,
    operation: MessageTemplateOperation, context: TemplateManagementContext, summary: TemplateSummary, recoveryOwnerId: string): Promise<void> {
    try { await this.transactions.run('record-outcome', async (tx) => {
      await this.assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx, actorId, draft);
      const actual = await this.resolver.resolve({ buId: context.buId, channelId: context.channelId,
        expectedGeneration: context.generation }, { transaction: tx });
      this.assertSameContext(context, actual.context);
      const [reservation, lockedDraft] = await Promise.all([
        tx.messageTemplateNameReservation.findUnique({ where: { operationId: operation.id }, select: { operationId: true } }),
        tx.messageTemplateDraft.findFirst({ where: { id: draft.id, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
          revision: operation.revision, state: 'LOCKED' }, select: { id: true } }),
      ]);
      if (!reservation || !lockedDraft) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RECONCILIATION_SUPERSEDED' });
      const [{ now }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
      const changed = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.updateMany({ where: {
        id: operation.id, draftId: draft.id, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
        actorUserId: operation.actorUserId, phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', dispatchClaimId: operation.dispatchClaimId,
        recoveryOwnerId, recoveryLeaseUntil: { gt: now }, version: operation.version + 1,
        requestHash: operation.requestHash, accountReference: context.accountReference,
        channelId: context.channelId, providerResourceIdentityId: context.providerResourceIdentityId,
        channelGeneration: context.generation, pairingEpoch: context.pairingEpoch,
        activeCredentialVersion: context.activeCredentialVersion,
      }, data: { phase: 'ACCEPTED', safeCode: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION', remoteTemplateId: summary.remoteTemplateId,
        acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION', recoveryOwnerId: null, recoveryLeaseUntil: null,
        leaseUntil: null, version: { increment: 1 } } });
      if (changed.count !== 1) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RECONCILIATION_SUPERSEDED' });
      const last = await tx.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.aggregate({ where: { operationId: operation.id }, _max: { sequence: true } });
      await tx.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.create({ data: { operationId: operation.id,
        sequence: (last._max.sequence ?? 0) + 1, phase: 'ACCEPTED', safeCode: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION',
        remoteTemplateId: summary.remoteTemplateId, acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' } });
      await this.registrarAtividade(tx, actorId, draft.mantenedoraId, draft.buId,
        operation.id, 'template.operacao.reconciliada');
    }); } catch (error) {
      let current: { phase: string; actorUserId: string; requestHash: string; accountReference: string; channelId: string;
        providerResourceIdentityId: string; channelGeneration: number; pairingEpoch: number; activeCredentialVersion: number | null;
        acceptanceProvenance: string | null; remoteTemplateId: string | null } | null;
      let receipt: { phase: string; safeCode: string | null; remoteTemplateId: string | null; acceptanceProvenance: string | null } | null;
      try { [current, receipt] = await Promise.all([
        this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUnique({ where: { id: operation.id }, select: {
          phase: true, actorUserId: true, requestHash: true, accountReference: true, channelId: true,
          providerResourceIdentityId: true, channelGeneration: true, pairingEpoch: true, activeCredentialVersion: true,
          acceptanceProvenance: true, remoteTemplateId: true,
        } }),
        this.prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findFirst({ where: { operationId: operation.id }, orderBy: [{ sequence: 'desc' }, { id: 'desc' }], select: {
          phase: true, safeCode: true, remoteTemplateId: true, acceptanceProvenance: true,
        } }),
      ]); } catch { throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', phase: 'ACCEPTED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' }); }
      const exact = current?.phase === 'ACCEPTED' && current.actorUserId === operation.actorUserId
        && current.requestHash === operation.requestHash && current.accountReference === context.accountReference
        && current.channelId === context.channelId && current.providerResourceIdentityId === context.providerResourceIdentityId
        && current.channelGeneration === context.generation && current.pairingEpoch === context.pairingEpoch
        && current.activeCredentialVersion === context.activeCredentialVersion
        && current.acceptanceProvenance === 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' && current.remoteTemplateId === summary.remoteTemplateId
        && receipt?.phase === 'ACCEPTED' && receipt.safeCode === 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION'
        && receipt.remoteTemplateId === summary.remoteTemplateId && receipt.acceptanceProvenance === 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION';
      if (exact) return;
      if (error instanceof ConflictException) throw error;
      throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE',
        operationId: operation.id, phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN',
        acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
    }
  }

  private async authorizeDraft(actorId: string, draftId: string, requireOwner: boolean) {
    const actor = await this.prisma.appUser.findUnique({ where: { id: actorId }, select: { mantenedoraId: true, status: true } });
    if (!actor || actor.status !== 'ATIVO') throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND' });
    const grants = await this.access.grantsDoUsuario(actorId);
    const grantedBuIds = grants.map((grant) => grant.buId);
    if (!grantedBuIds.length) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND' });
    const draft = await this.prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findFirst({ where: {
      id: draftId, mantenedoraId: actor.mantenedoraId, buId: { in: grantedBuIds },
    } });
    if (!draft) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND' });
    const grant = grants.find((row) => row.buId === draft.buId);
    if (!grant) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND' });
    if (requireOwner && draft.creatorUserId !== actorId && !atLeast(grant.role, 'ADMIN')) {
      throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND' });
    }
    return { actor, draft, role: grant.role as Role };
  }

  private async assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, actorId: string, draft: MessageTemplateDraft): Promise<void> {
    const [actor, bu, grant] = await Promise.all([
      tx.appUser.findFirst({ where: { id: actorId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId, status: 'ATIVO' }, select: { id: true } }),
      tx.businessUnit.findFirst({ where: { id: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId, status: 'ATIVO' }, select: { id: true } }),
      tx.userBuRole.findUnique({ where: { userId_buId: { userId: actorId, buId: draft.buId } }, select: { role: true } }),
    ]);
    if (!actor || !bu || !grant || (draft.creatorUserId !== actorId && !atLeast(grant.role, 'ADMIN'))) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_AUTHORIZATION_CHANGED' });
    }
  }

  private async preflightName(provider: MessageTemplateProvider, context: TemplateManagementContext, definition: TemplateDefinition): Promise<void> {
    let after: string | null = null;
    for (let pageNumber = 0; pageNumber < 2; pageNumber += 1) {
      const page = await provider.listTemplates({ context, name: definition.name, after, limit: 50 });
      for (const summary of page.templates) {
        if (summary.name !== definition.name || !summary.language) {
          throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE' });
        }
        if (summary.language === definition.language) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_NAME_UNAVAILABLE' });
      }
      if (!page.nextCursor) return;
      after = page.nextCursor;
    }
    throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE' });
  }

  private async admitActorAttempt(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, actorId: string): Promise<void> {
    const scopeKey = `actor:${actorId}`;
    await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT 1 AS locked FROM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${scopeKey}, 0))`;
    const [{ now }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
    const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - ACTOR_WINDOW_MS);
    const count = await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'ACTOR', scopeKey, occurredAt: { gt: cutoff } } });
    if (count >= 10) throw new HttpException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RATE_LIMITED', retryAfter: 60 }, 429);
    await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.deleteMany({ where: { kind: 'ACTOR', scopeKey, occurredAt: { lte: cutoff } } });
    await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.create({ data: { kind: 'ACTOR', scopeKey, operationId: null, occurredAt: now } });
  }

  private async reserveAccountDispatch(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, accountReference: string, operationId: string): Promise<Date> {
    const scopeKey = `account:${accountReference}`;
    await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT 1 AS locked FROM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${scopeKey}, 0))`;
    const [{ now: dbNow }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
    const cutoff = new Date(dbNow.getTime() - ACCOUNT_WINDOW_MS);
    const count = await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'ACCOUNT', scopeKey, occurredAt: { gt: cutoff } } });
    if (count >= 60) throw new HttpException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_RATE_LIMITED', retryAfter: 3600 }, 429);
    await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.deleteMany({ where: { kind: 'ACCOUNT', scopeKey, occurredAt: { lte: cutoff } } });
    await tx.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.create({ data: { kind: 'ACCOUNT', scopeKey, operationId, occurredAt: dbNow } });
    return dbNow;
  }

  private async finishRejected(operationId: string, ownerId: string, dispatchClaimId: string | undefined, phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: string, dispatched: boolean, actorId: string, buId: string, mantenedoraId: string) {
    try {
      await this.transactions.run('release', async (tx) => {
        const changed = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.updateMany({
          where: { id: operationId, phase: dispatchClaimId ? 'SENDING' : 'PREPARED', leaseOwnerId: ownerId,
            ...(dispatchClaimId ? { dispatchClaimId } : {}) },
          data: { phase, safeCode, version: { increment: 1 }, leaseUntil: null },
        });
        if (changed.count !== 1) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_SUPERSEDED' });
        await tx.messageTemplateNameReservation.deleteMany({ where: { operationId } });
        const operation = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId }, select: { draftId: true, revision: true } });
        await tx.messageTemplateDraft.updateMany({ where: { id: operation.draftId, revision: operation.revision, state: 'LOCKED' }, data: { state: 'DRAFT' } });

  private async claimReconciliation(actorId: string, draft: MessageTemplateDraft, operation: MessageTemplateOperation, recoveryOwnerId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      return await this.transactions.run('reconcile-claim', async (tx) => {
        await this.assertTxOwnerAdmin(tx, actorId, draft);
        const [{ now }] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ now: Date }>>`SELECT clock_timestamp() AS now`;
        const changed = await tx.messageTemplateOperation.updateMany({ where: {
          id: operation.id, draftId: draft.id, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,
          actorUserId: operation.actorUserId, phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', version: operation.version,
          OR: [{ recoveryLeaseUntil: null }, { recoveryLeaseUntil: { lte: now } }],
        }, data: { recoveryOwnerId, recoveryLeaseUntil: new Date(now.getTime() + LEASE_MS), version: { increment: 1 } } });
        return changed.count === 1;
      });

      const readback = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
        id: operation.id, draftId: draft.id, buId: draft.buId, mantenedoraId: draft.mantenedoraId,

        recoveryLeaseUntil: { gt: new Date() }, version: operation.version + 1,
      }, select: { id: true } }).catch(() => null);
      if (readback) return true;
      const now = new Date();
      const current = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
        id: operation.id, actorUserId: operation.actorUserId, phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', recoveryLeaseUntil: { gt: now },

  private async projectOperation(operationId: string, buId: string, mantenedoraId: string) {
    const operation = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({
      where: { id: operationId, buId, mantenedoraId },
    });
    if (!operation) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_OPERATION_NOT_FOUND' });
    const receipts = await this.prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({
      where: { operationId: operation.id }, orderBy: { sequence: 'desc' }, take: 10,
      select: { id: true, sequence: true, phase: true, safeCode: true, remoteTemplateId: true,
        acceptanceProvenance: true, createdAt: true },
    });
    const savedObservation = operation.remoteTemplateId
      ? await this.observations.projectSavedObservation({ mantenedoraId: operation.mantenedoraId,
        buId: operation.buId, channelId: operation.channelId,
        providerResourceIdentityId: operation.providerResourceIdentityId,
        accountReference: operation.accountReference, generation: operation.channelGeneration,
        pairingEpoch: operation.pairingEpoch, activeCredentialVersion: operation.activeCredentialVersion },
      operation.remoteTemplateId, operation.definitionSnapshot, operation.acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId)
      : null;
    return Object.freeze({
      id: operation.id, phase: operation.phase, revision: operation.revision,
      providerAcceptance: operation.acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId ? 'CONFIRMED' : 'UNKNOWN',
      acceptanceProvenance: operation.acceptanceProvenance === 'ACKNOWLEDGED' ? 'ACKNOWLEDGED'
        : operation.acceptanceProvenance === 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' ? 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' : undefined,
      receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' as const,
      remoteTemplateId: operation.remoteTemplateId ?? null, safeCode: operation.safeCode ?? null,
      savedObservation,
      createdAt: operation.createdAt, updatedAt: operation.updatedAt,
      leaseExpiresAt: operation.recoveryLeaseUntil ?? operation.leaseUntil ?? null,
      recoveryAction: ['PREPARED', 'SENDING', 'AMBIGUOUS'].includes(operation.phase)
        ? ((operation.recoveryLeaseUntil ?? operation.leaseUntil)?.getTime() ?? 0) > Date.now() ? 'WAIT' : 'RECONCILE' : null,
      receipts: receipts.map((receipt) => ({ id: receipt.id, sequence: receipt.sequence,
        phase: receipt.phase, safeCode: receipt.safeCode ?? null,
        remoteTemplateId: receipt.remoteTemplateId ?? null,
        acceptanceProvenance: receipt.acceptanceProvenance ?? undefined, createdAt: receipt.createdAt })),

  private async readback(operationId: string, actorId: string, buId: string, mantenedoraId: string) {
    const operation = await this.prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({ where: {
      id: operationId, actorUserId: actorId, buId, mantenedoraId,
    } });
    if (!operation) return { operation: null, reservation: null, draft: null };
    const [joined] = await this.prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
      draftId: string; draftBuId: string; draftMantenedoraId: string; draftState: string; draftRevision: number;
      reservationOperationId: string | null; reservationAccountReference: string | null;
      reservationName: string | null; reservationLanguage: string | null;
      receiptPhase: string | null; receiptSafeCode: string | null;
    }>>`
      SELECT d.id AS "draftId", d.bu_id AS "draftBuId", d.mantenedora_id AS "draftMantenedoraId",
        d.state::text AS "draftState", d.revision AS "draftRevision",
        r.operation_id AS "reservationOperationId", r.account_reference AS "reservationAccountReference",
        r.name AS "reservationName", r.language AS "reservationLanguage",
        receipt.phase::text AS "receiptPhase", receipt.safe_code AS "receiptSafeCode"
      FROM message_template_drafts d
      LEFT JOIN message_template_name_reservations r ON r.operation_id = ${operationId}
      LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT phase, safe_code FROM message_template_submission_receipts
        WHERE operation_id = ${operationId}
        ORDER BY sequence DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
      ) receipt ON TRUE
      WHERE d.id = ${operation.draftId} AND d.bu_id = ${buId} AND d.mantenedora_id = ${mantenedoraId}
      LIMIT 1`;
    const draft = joined ? { id: joined.draftId, buId: joined.draftBuId,
      mantenedoraId: joined.draftMantenedoraId, state: joined.draftState, revision: joined.draftRevision } : null;
    const reservation = joined?.reservationOperationId ? {
      operationId: joined.reservationOperationId, accountReference: joined.reservationAccountReference,
      name: joined.reservationName, language: joined.reservationLanguage,
    } : null;
    const receipt = joined?.receiptPhase ? [{ phase: joined.receiptPhase, safeCode: joined.receiptSafeCode }] : [];
    return { operation: { ...operation, receipts: receipt }, reservation, draft };
  }

  private assertBinding(draft: MessageTemplateDraft, context: TemplateManagementContext): void {
    if (draft.mantenedoraId !== context.mantenedoraId || draft.channelId !== context.channelId || draft.providerResourceIdentityId !== context.providerResourceIdentityId) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_CHANNEL_CONTEXT_STALE' });
    }
    if (draft.accountReference !== context.accountReference) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CONTA_ALTERADA' });
  }

  private assertSameContext(expected: TemplateManagementContext, actual: TemplateManagementContext): void {
    if (expected.accountReference !== actual.accountReference) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CONTA_ALTERADA' });
    if (expected.mantenedoraId !== actual.mantenedoraId || expected.buId !== actual.buId || expected.channelId !== actual.channelId
      || expected.providerResourceIdentityId !== actual.providerResourceIdentityId || expected.generation !== actual.generation
      || expected.pairingEpoch !== actual.pairingEpoch || expected.activeCredentialVersion !== actual.activeCredentialVersion) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_CHANNEL_CONTEXT_STALE' });
    }
  }

  private hash(value: unknown): string { return createHash('sha256').update(this.canonicalJson(value)).digest('hex'); }
  private async registrarAtividade(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, actorId: string, mantenedoraId: string,
    buId: string, entidadeId: string,
    action: 'template.submissao.iniciada' | 'template.operacao.reconciliada'): Promise<void> {
    const actor = await tx.appUser.findFirst({ where: { id: actorId, mantenedoraId, status: 'ATIVO' },
      select: { id: true, name: true, email: true } });
    if (!actor) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_OPERATION_NOT_FOUND' });
    await this.registro.registrar(tx, mantenedoraId, action,
      { id: actor.id, nome: actor.name, email: actor.email }, { buId, entidadeId });
  }

  private canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map((item) => this.canonicalJson(item)).join(',')}]`;
    if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
      const row = value as Record<string, unknown>;
      return `{${Object.keys(row).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${this.canonicalJson(row[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
    }
    return JSON.stringify(value);
  }
  private isUnique(error: unknown): boolean { return error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && error.code === 'P2002'; }

  private statusOf(error: unknown): number | undefined {
    return error instanceof HttpException ? error.getStatus() : undefined;
  }

  private isExactPrepared(
    state: Awaited<ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['readback']>> | null,
    operationId: string,
    ownerId: string,
    actorId: string,
    draft: MessageTemplateDraft,
    requestHash: string,
    context: TemplateManagementContext,
    definition: TemplateDefinition,
  ): boolean {
    return Boolean(state?.operation?.id === operationId && state.operation.phase === 'PREPARED'
      && state.operation.actorUserId === actorId && state.operation.buId === draft.buId
      && state.operation.mantenedoraId === draft.mantenedoraId && state.operation.draftId === draft.id
      && state.operation.requestHash === requestHash && state.operation.leaseOwnerId === ownerId
      && state.operation.channelId === context.channelId
      && state.operation.providerResourceIdentityId === context.providerResourceIdentityId
      && state.operation.accountReference === context.accountReference
      && state.operation.channelGeneration === context.generation
      && state.operation.pairingEpoch === context.pairingEpoch
      && state.operation.activeCredentialVersion === context.activeCredentialVersion
      && state.operation.receipts[0]?.phase === 'PREPARED'
      && state.operation.receipts[0]?.safeCode === 'RESERVED'
      && state.operation.leaseUntil && state.operation.leaseUntil.getTime() > Date.now()
      && state.reservation?.operationId === operationId && state.draft?.state === 'LOCKED'
      && state.reservation.accountReference === context.accountReference
      && state.reservation.name === definition.name && state.reservation.language === definition.language
      && state.draft.id === draft.id && state.draft.buId === draft.buId
      && state.draft.mantenedoraId === draft.mantenedoraId
      && state.draft.revision === draft.revision);
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { PrismaService } from '../../infra/prisma/prisma.service';

export type TemplateTransactionPhase = 'actor-admission' | 'reserve' | 'claim' | 'record-outcome' | 'release' | 'reconcile-claim' | 'reconcile-release' | 'observation';
export const TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY = Symbol('TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY');

/** A fault-injectable boundary that always delegates to a real Prisma transaction. */
export interface TemplateTransactionBoundary {
  run<T>(phase: TemplateTransactionPhase, work: (tx: Prisma.TransactionClient) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

@Injectable()
export class PrismaTemplateTransactionBoundary implements TemplateTransactionBoundary {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  run<T>(_phase: TemplateTransactionPhase, work: (tx: Prisma.TransactionClient) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    return this.prisma.$transaction(work);
  }
}

import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { PrismaService } from '../../infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../../domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import type {
  MessageTemplateProvider,
  ResolvedTemplateManagement,
  TemplateAccountReference,
  TemplateManagementContext,
  TemplateManagementResolver,
  TemplateReference,
} from '../../domain/message-templates/template-management.ports';
import { BoundMetaMessageTemplateProvider, MetaTemplateClient, TemplateManagementProviderError } from '../meta/message-template.client';

interface TemplateProviderFactory {
  create(context: TemplateManagementContext, credential: () => Promise<string>, wabaId: string): MessageTemplateProvider;
}

class OfficialTemplateProviderFactory implements TemplateProviderFactory {
  create(context: TemplateManagementContext, credential: () => Promise<string>, wabaId: string): MessageTemplateProvider {
    return new BoundMetaMessageTemplateProvider(context, credential, wabaId, new MetaTemplateClient());
  }
}

/** Explicit allowlist: unsupported origins have no placeholder/no-op provider. */
class TemplateProviderFactoryRegistry {
  private readonly factories = new Map([['OFICIAL', new OfficialTemplateProviderFactory()]]);

  create(origin: string, context: TemplateManagementContext, credential: () => Promise<string>, wabaId: string): MessageTemplateProvider {
    const factory = this.factories.get(origin);
    if (!factory) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    return factory.create(context, credential, wabaId);
  }
}

@Injectable()
export class ScopedTemplateManagementResolver implements TemplateManagementResolver {
  private readonly factories = new TemplateProviderFactoryRegistry();

  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService, private readonly credentials: ChannelCredentialResolver) {}

  async resolve(reference: TemplateReference, options?: { readonly transaction?: Prisma.TransactionClient }): Promise<ResolvedTemplateManagement> {
    const database = options?.transaction ?? this.prisma;
    const channel = await database.buChannel.findFirst({
      where: { id: reference.channelId, buId: reference.buId },
      include: { providerIdentities: true, bu: { select: { mantenedoraId: true } } },
    });
    if (!channel || channel.generation !== reference.expectedGeneration || channel.type !== 'OFICIAL'
      || !channel.ownNumberVerified || channel.verifiedEpoch !== channel.pairingEpoch) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    }
    const identity = channel.providerIdentities.find((row) => row.currentChannelId === channel.id);
    if (!identity || !identity.managedLifecycle || identity.provider !== 'OFICIAL' || identity.buId !== channel.buId
      || !channel.phoneNumberId || identity.externalResourceId !== channel.phoneNumberId || !channel.wabaId) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    }
    const credentialMetadata = await this.credentials.inspectActiveCredential(channel.id, channel.generation, { provider: 'OFICIAL', buId: channel.buId }, options?.transaction);
    if (!credentialMetadata.ready || credentialMetadata.version === null) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    }
    const context: TemplateManagementContext = Object.freeze({
      mantenedoraId: channel.bu.mantenedoraId,
      buId: channel.buId,
      channelId: channel.id,
      providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id,
      generation: channel.generation,
      pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch,
      activeCredentialVersion: credentialMetadata.version,
      accountReference: this.accountReference(channel.wabaId),
    });
    const credential = async (): Promise<string> => {
      const current = await this.credentials.resolve(channel.id, 'template-management');
      if (current.provider !== 'OFICIAL' || current.buId !== context.buId || current.channelId !== context.channelId
        || current.providerResourceIdentityId !== context.providerResourceIdentityId || current.generation !== context.generation
        || current.pairingEpoch !== context.pairingEpoch || current.activeCredentialVersion !== context.activeCredentialVersion
        || current.wabaId !== channel.wabaId || current.phoneNumberId !== channel.phoneNumberId
        || this.accountReference(current.wabaId ?? '') !== context.accountReference || !current.accessToken) {
        throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
      }
      return current.accessToken;
    };
    const provider = this.factories.create(channel.type, context, credential, channel.wabaId);
    return Object.freeze({ context, provider });
  }

  private accountReference(wabaId: string): TemplateAccountReference {
    return createHash('sha256').update(`meta-waba\0${wabaId}`).digest('hex') as TemplateAccountReference;
  }
}

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import type {
  MessageTemplateProvider,
  TemplateComponentSummary,
  TemplateCreateOutcome,
  TemplateDefinition,
  TemplateDispatchGuard,
  TemplateManagementContext,
  TemplatePage,
  TemplateSummary,
} from '../../domain/message-templates/template-management.ports';

const PROVEN_REFUSAL_CODES = new Set([10, 100, 190, 2388040, 2388047, 2388072, 2388073, 2388293, 2388299]);
const PROVIDER_FIELDS = 'id,name,language,status,category,parameter_format,components';
const OPERATION_BUDGET_MS = 45_000;
const MAX_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_BYTES = 1024 * 1024;

export class TemplateManagementProviderError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE' | 'TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE') {
    super(code === 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE'
      ? 'A autorização atual não permite gerenciar modelos.'
      : 'A resposta do provedor de modelos está indisponível.');
    this.name = 'TemplateManagementProviderError';
  }
}

type GraphError = { readonly type: string; readonly message: string; readonly code: number; readonly is_transient?: boolean };
type Grant = { readonly appId: string; readonly valid: boolean; readonly expiresAt?: number; readonly scopes: readonly string[]; readonly targets: readonly string[] };

class RequestNotDispatchedError extends Error {}

/** Private, operation-scoped Graph client. One instance belongs to one resolved context. */
export class MetaTemplateClient {
  private readonly appId = process.env.META_APP_ID?.trim() ?? '';
  private readonly appSecret = process.env.META_APP_SECRET ?? '';
  private readonly graphVersion = process.env.GRAPH_VERSION?.trim() ?? '';
  private startedAt: number | null = null;
  private grantPromise: Promise<Grant> | null = null;
  private boundToken: string | null = null;
  private boundWaba: string | null = null;

  async createTemplate(input: { accessToken: string; wabaId: string; definition: TemplateDefinition; dispatchGuard?: TemplateDispatchGuard }): Promise<TemplateCreateOutcome> {
    const { accessToken, wabaId, definition } = input;
    try { await this.grant(accessToken, wabaId); } catch {
      return { kind: 'REJECTED', dispatched: false, code: 'PRE_DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE' };
    }
    const components: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
    if (definition.header !== undefined) components.push({ type: 'HEADER', format: 'TEXT', text: definition.header });
    const namedExamples = Object.keys(definition.examples).sort().map((paramName) => ({
      param_name: paramName,
      example: definition.examples[paramName],
    }));
    components.push({
      type: 'BODY', text: definition.body,
      ...(namedExamples.length ? { example: { body_text_named_params: namedExamples } } : {}),
    });
    if (definition.footer !== undefined) components.push({ type: 'FOOTER', text: definition.footer });

    let response: Response;
    let payload: unknown;
    try {
      try { await input.dispatchGuard?.beforeDispatch(); } catch { throw new RequestNotDispatchedError(); }
      response = await this.request(`/${encodeURIComponent(wabaId)}/message_templates`, accessToken, 'POST', {
        name: definition.name, language: definition.language, category: definition.category,
        parameter_format: 'NAMED', components,
      });
      payload = await this.readJson(response);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof RequestNotDispatchedError) return { kind: 'REJECTED', dispatched: false, code: 'PRE_DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE' };
      return { kind: 'AMBIGUOUS', dispatched: true, code: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN' };
    }
    if (!isRecord(payload)) return { kind: 'AMBIGUOUS', dispatched: true, code: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN' };
    const hasGraphError = payload.error !== undefined;
    const validId = typeof payload.id === 'string' && payload.id.trim().length > 0 && payload.id.length <= 128;
    const hasAnyId = Object.hasOwn(payload, 'id');
    const contradicted = Object.hasOwn(payload, 'success') || Object.hasOwn(payload, 'data') || Object.hasOwn(payload, 'messages');
    if (response.ok && validId && !hasGraphError && !contradicted) {
      return { kind: 'ACCEPTED', dispatched: true, remoteTemplateId: payload.id as string };
    }
    const graphError = parseGraphError(payload.error);
    if ([400, 401, 403].includes(response.status) && graphError && PROVEN_REFUSAL_CODES.has(graphError.code)
      && graphError.is_transient !== true && !hasAnyId && !contradicted) {
      return { kind: 'REJECTED', dispatched: true, provenNoCreation: true, code: 'PROVIDER_REFUSED' };
    }
    return { kind: 'AMBIGUOUS', dispatched: true, code: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN' };
  }

  async listTemplates(input: {
    readonly context: TemplateManagementContext;
    readonly accessToken: string;
    readonly wabaId: string;
    readonly name?: string;
    readonly after: string | null;
    readonly limit: number;
  }): Promise<TemplatePage> {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.limit) || input.limit < 1 || input.limit > 50
      || (input.name !== undefined && (input.name.length < 1 || input.name.length > 128))
      || (input.after !== null && (input.after.length < 1 || input.after.length > 512))) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    await this.grant(input.accessToken, input.wabaId);
    const templates: TemplateSummary[] = [];
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ fields: PROVIDER_FIELDS, limit: String(input.limit) });
    if (input.after) query.set('after', input.after);
    if (input.name) query.set('name', input.name);
    const result = await this.readObject(await this.request(`/${encodeURIComponent(input.wabaId)}/message_templates?${query}`, input.accessToken));
    if (!Array.isArray(result.data) || result.data.length > input.limit) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    for (const row of result.data) {
      const summary = parseTemplateSummary(row);
      if (input.name !== undefined && summary.name !== input.name) {
        throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
      }
      templates.push(summary);
    }
    return { templates, nextCursor: parseNextCursor(result.paging) };
  }

  async getTemplate(input: {
    readonly context: TemplateManagementContext;
    readonly accessToken: string;
    readonly wabaId: string;
    readonly remoteTemplateId: string;
  }): Promise<TemplateSummary> {
    if (!input.remoteTemplateId || input.remoteTemplateId.length > 128) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    await this.grant(input.accessToken, input.wabaId);
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ fields: PROVIDER_FIELDS });
    const result = await this.request(`/${encodeURIComponent(input.remoteTemplateId)}?${query}`, input.accessToken);
    const summary = parseTemplateSummary(await this.readObject(result));
    if (summary.remoteTemplateId !== input.remoteTemplateId) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    return summary;
  }

  private async grant(token: string, wabaId: string): Promise<Grant> {
    if (!token || !wabaId || wabaId.length > 128 || !this.appId || !this.appSecret || !/^v\d+\.0$/.test(this.graphVersion)) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    if (this.boundToken !== null && (this.boundToken !== token || this.boundWaba !== wabaId)) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    this.boundToken = token;
    this.boundWaba = wabaId;
    this.grantPromise ??= this.loadGrant(token, wabaId);
    return this.grantPromise;
  }

  private async loadGrant(token: string, wabaId: string): Promise<Grant> {
    let payload: Record<string, unknown>;
    try {
      const query = new URLSearchParams({ input_token: token });
      const result = await this.request(`/debug_token?${query}`, `${this.appId}|${this.appSecret}`);
      const wrapper = await this.readObject(result);
      if (wrapper.error !== undefined || !isRecord(wrapper.data)) throw new Error('invalid');
      payload = wrapper.data;
    } catch {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    const appId = typeof payload.app_id === 'string' ? payload.app_id : '';
    const valid = payload.is_valid === true;
    if ((Object.hasOwn(payload, 'expires_at') && typeof payload.expires_at !== 'number')
      || (Object.hasOwn(payload, 'data_access_expires_at') && typeof payload.data_access_expires_at !== 'number')) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    const scopes = Array.isArray(payload.scopes) && payload.scopes.every((item) => typeof item === 'string')
      ? payload.scopes as string[] : [];
    const granular = Array.isArray(payload.granular_scopes) ? payload.granular_scopes : [];
    const targets = granular.flatMap((entry) => isRecord(entry) && entry.scope === 'whatsapp_business_management'
      && Array.isArray(entry.target_ids) && entry.target_ids.every((item) => typeof item === 'string')
      ? entry.target_ids as string[] : []);
    const expiresAt = typeof payload.expires_at === 'number' ? payload.expires_at : undefined;
    const dataExpiresAt = typeof payload.data_access_expires_at === 'number' ? payload.data_access_expires_at : undefined;
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const expiryValid = (expiresAt === undefined || expiresAt === 0 || expiresAt > now)
      && (dataExpiresAt === undefined || dataExpiresAt === 0 || dataExpiresAt > now);
    if (appId !== this.appId || !valid || !scopes.includes('whatsapp_business_management')
      || !targets.includes(wabaId) || !expiryValid) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    return { appId, valid, expiresAt: expiresAt ?? dataExpiresAt, scopes, targets };
  }

  private async request(path: string, accessToken?: string, method: 'GET' | 'POST' = 'GET', body?: unknown): Promise<Response> {
    const startedAt = this.startedAt ?? (this.startedAt = Date.now());
    const remainingMs = OPERATION_BUDGET_MS - (Date.now() - startedAt);
    if (remainingMs <= 0 || !/^v\d+\.0$/.test(this.graphVersion)) {
      throw new RequestNotDispatchedError();
    }
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { accept: 'application/json' };
    if (accessToken) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
    if (body !== undefined) headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
    try {
      return await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/${this.graphVersion}${path}`, {
        method, headers, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(1, Math.min(10_000, remainingMs))), redirect: 'error',
      });
    } catch {
      throw new Error('unavailable');
    }
  }

  private async readJson(response: Response): Promise<unknown> {
    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    if (!reader) return null;
    const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
    let byteLength = 0;
    try {
      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        byteLength += value.byteLength;
        if (byteLength > MAX_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_BYTES) {
          await reader.cancel();
          return null;
        }
        chunks.push(value);
      }
      return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(Buffer.concat(chunks, byteLength))) as unknown;
    } catch {
      try { await reader.cancel(); } catch { /* body already aborted */ }
      return null;
    }
  }

  private async readObject(response: Response): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    if (!response.ok) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    const payload = await this.readJson(response);
    if (!isRecord(payload) || Object.hasOwn(payload, 'error')) {
      throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    return payload;
  }
}

/** Adapter facade only; all network policy and translation stay in MetaTemplateClient. */
export class BoundMetaMessageTemplateProvider implements MessageTemplateProvider {
  constructor(
    private readonly context: TemplateManagementContext,
    private readonly accessToken: () => Promise<string>,
    private readonly wabaId: string,
    private readonly client: MetaTemplateClient,
  ) {}

  async createTemplate(input: { context: TemplateManagementContext; definition: TemplateDefinition; dispatchGuard?: TemplateDispatchGuard }): Promise<TemplateCreateOutcome> {
    this.assertContext(input.context);
    let accessToken: string;
    try { accessToken = await this.accessToken(); }
    catch { return { kind: 'REJECTED', dispatched: false, code: 'PRE_DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE' }; }
    return this.client.createTemplate({ accessToken, wabaId: this.wabaId, definition: input.definition, dispatchGuard: input.dispatchGuard });
  }

  async listTemplates(input: { context: TemplateManagementContext; name?: string; after: string | null; limit: number }): Promise<TemplatePage> {
    this.assertContext(input.context);
    const accessToken = await this.accessToken();
    return this.client.listTemplates({ ...input, context: this.context, accessToken, wabaId: this.wabaId });
  }

  async getTemplate(input: { context: TemplateManagementContext; remoteTemplateId: string }): Promise<TemplateSummary> {
    this.assertContext(input.context);
    const accessToken = await this.accessToken();
    return this.client.getTemplate({ ...input, context: this.context, accessToken, wabaId: this.wabaId });
  }

  private assertContext(context: TemplateManagementContext): void {
    if (!sameContext(this.context, context)) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
  }
}

function parseGraphError(value: unknown): GraphError | null {
  if (!isRecord(value) || typeof value.type !== 'string' || value.type.trim().length === 0
    || typeof value.message !== 'string' || value.message.trim().length === 0
    || typeof value.code !== 'number' || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.code) || value.code < 0
    || (value.is_transient !== undefined && typeof value.is_transient !== 'boolean')) return null;
  return { type: value.type, message: value.message, code: value.code, ...(typeof value.is_transient === 'boolean' ? { is_transient: value.is_transient } : {}) };
}

function parseTemplateSummary(value: unknown): TemplateSummary {
  if (!isRecord(value) || typeof value.id !== 'string' || value.id.trim().length === 0 || value.id.length > 128) {
    throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  }
  const statusValues = new Set(['PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'PAUSED', 'DISABLED', 'ARCHIVED', 'DELETED', 'IN_APPEAL', 'PENDING_DELETION', 'LIMIT_EXCEEDED']);
  const categoryValues = new Set(['MARKETING', 'UTILITY', 'AUTHENTICATION']);
  if (Array.isArray(value.components) && value.components.length > 16) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  const components: TemplateComponentSummary[] = Array.isArray(value.components) ? value.components.map(parseComponent) : [{ type: 'UNKNOWN', providerType: 'UNKNOWN', semanticFingerprint: createHash('sha256').update('missing-components').digest('hex') }];
  const summary: Omit<TemplateSummary, 'supported' | 'semanticFingerprint'> = {
    remoteTemplateId: value.id,
    status: typeof value.status === 'string' && statusValues.has(value.status) ? value.status as TemplateSummary['status'] : 'UNKNOWN',
    name: typeof value.name === 'string' && characterCount(value.name) > 0 && characterCount(value.name) <= 128 ? value.name : null,
    language: typeof value.language === 'string' && characterCount(value.language) > 0 && characterCount(value.language) <= 35 ? value.language : null,
    category: typeof value.category === 'string' && categoryValues.has(value.category) ? value.category as TemplateSummary['category'] : 'UNKNOWN',
    parameterFormat: value.parameter_format === 'NAMED' || value.parameter_format === 'POSITIONAL' ? value.parameter_format : 'UNKNOWN',
    components,
  };
  const bodies = components.filter((component) => component.type === 'BODY');
  const headers = components.filter((component) => component.type === 'HEADER');
  const footers = components.filter((component) => component.type === 'FOOTER');
  const noUnknown = components.every((component) => component.type !== 'UNKNOWN');
  const headerSupported = headers.every((component) => component.type === 'HEADER' && component.format === 'TEXT');
  const namedParameters = components.every((component) => component.type === 'UNKNOWN'
    || !('parameters' in component) || component.parameters.every((parameter) => !/^[1-9][0-9]{0,1}$/.test(parameter)));
  const supportable = summary.name !== null && summary.language !== null
    && (summary.category === 'UTILITY' || summary.category === 'MARKETING')
    && summary.parameterFormat === 'NAMED' && bodies.length === 1 && headers.length <= 1
    && footers.length <= 1 && noUnknown && headerSupported && namedParameters
    && bodies[0]?.type === 'BODY' && bodies[0].text.trim().length > 0;
  const semanticFingerprint = supportable ? createHash('sha256').update(canonicalSemanticJson({
      name: summary.name, language: summary.language, category: summary.category,
      parameterFormat: summary.parameterFormat, components: value.components,
    })).digest('hex') : null;
  return { ...summary, supported: supportable, semanticFingerprint };
}

function parseComponent(value: unknown): TemplateComponentSummary {
  if (!isRecord(value) || typeof value.type !== 'string') return unknownComponent(value, 'UNKNOWN');
  const supportedKeys = value.type === 'HEADER'
    ? new Set(['type', 'format', 'text', 'example'])
    : value.type === 'BODY' || value.type === 'FOOTER'
      ? new Set(['type', 'text', 'example'])
      : new Set(['type', 'example']);
  if (Object.keys(value).some((key) => !supportedKeys.has(key))) return unknownComponent(value, 'OTHER');
  if (value.type === 'HEADER') {
    if (value.format === 'TEXT' && typeof value.text === 'string' && characterCount(value.text) <= 60) {
      const parameters = parseRemoteParameters(value.text);
      if (parameters) return { type: 'HEADER', format: 'TEXT', text: value.text, parameters };
      return unknownComponent(value, 'OTHER');
    }
    if (value.text !== undefined) return unknownComponent(value, 'OTHER');
    const format = value.format === 'IMAGE' || value.format === 'VIDEO' || value.format === 'DOCUMENT' ? value.format : 'UNKNOWN';
    if (format === 'UNKNOWN') return unknownComponent(value, 'OTHER');
    return { type: 'HEADER', format, text: null };
  }
  if ((value.type === 'BODY' || value.type === 'FOOTER') && typeof value.text === 'string'
    && characterCount(value.text) <= (value.type === 'BODY' ? 1024 : 60)) {
    const parameters = parseRemoteParameters(value.text);
    if (parameters && (value.type !== 'FOOTER' || parameters.length === 0)) return { type: value.type, text: value.text, parameters };
  }
  return unknownComponent(value, 'OTHER');
}

function parseRemoteParameters(text: string): string[] | null {
  const tokens: string[] = [];
  const pattern = /\{\{([^{}]+)\}\}/g;
  for (const match of text.matchAll(pattern)) {
    if (!/^(?:[a-z][a-z_]{0,31}|[1-9][0-9]{0,1})$/.test(match[1])) return null;
    tokens.push(match[1]);
  }
  if (/[{}]/.test(text.replace(pattern, ''))) return null;
  return tokens;
}

function characterCount(value: string): number {
  return Array.from(value).length;
}

function unknownComponent(value: unknown, providerType: 'OTHER' | 'UNKNOWN'): TemplateComponentSummary {
  return {
    type: 'UNKNOWN', providerType,
    semanticFingerprint: createHash('sha256').update(canonicalSemanticJson(value)).digest('hex'),
  };
}

function canonicalSemanticJson(value: unknown): string {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalSemanticJson).join(',')}]`;
  if (isRecord(value)) return `{${Object.keys(value).filter((key) => key !== 'example').sort()
    .map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalSemanticJson(value[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';
}

function parseNextCursor(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return null;
  if (!isRecord(value)) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  if (value.next === undefined || value.next === null || value.next === '') return null;
  if (typeof value.next !== 'string') throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  if (!isRecord(value.cursors)) throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  const after = value.cursors.after;
  if (typeof after !== 'string' || after.trim().length === 0 || after.length > 512) {
    throw new TemplateManagementProviderError('TEMPLATE_PROVIDER_RESPONSE_UNAVAILABLE');
  }
  return after;
}

function sameContext(a: TemplateManagementContext, b: TemplateManagementContext): boolean {
  return a.mantenedoraId === b.mantenedoraId && a.buId === b.buId && a.channelId === b.channelId && a.providerResourceIdentityId === b.providerResourceIdentityId
    && a.generation === b.generation && a.pairingEpoch === b.pairingEpoch
    && a.activeCredentialVersion === b.activeCredentialVersion && a.accountReference === b.accountReference;
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

import { ModulosService } from '../../domain/produto/modulos.service';

import { PainelService } from '../../domain/painel/painel.service';
import { ArmazenamentoDeArquivo, ArmazenamentoEmDisco } from '../../infra/arquivos/armazenamento';
import { EnvioDeEmail, ResendEmail } from '../../integrations/email/email.client';
import { TokensService } from '../../domain/acesso/tokens.service';
import { ConvitesService } from '../../domain/acesso/convites.service';
import { LimiteDeRecuperacao } from '../../domain/acesso/limite-de-recuperacao.service';
import { ChannelTransportModule } from '../../integrations/channels/channel-transport.module';
import { AuthController } from './auth.controller';
import { ChannelsController } from './channels.controller';
import { ConversationsController } from './conversations.controller';
import { UsuariosService } from '../../domain/identity/usuarios.service';
import { HistoricoController } from './historico.controller';
import { UsuariosController } from './usuarios.controller';
import { IntegracaoController } from './integracao.controller';
import { BusinessUnitsController, MeController } from './me.controller';
import { SetoresController } from './setores.controller';
import { AtividadesController } from './atividades.controller';
import { OnboardingController } from './onboarding.controller';
import { PainelController } from './painel.controller';
import { ArquivosController } from './arquivos.controller';
import { SessionMiddleware } from './session.middleware';
import { TYPEBOT_CLIENT, TypebotHttpClient } from '../../integrations/typebot/typebot.client';
import { TemplateManagementModule } from '../../integrations/message-templates/template-management.module';
import { TemplateDraftService } from '../../domain/message-templates/template-draft.service';
import { TemplateSubmissionService } from '../../domain/message-templates/template-submission.service';
import { PrismaTemplateTransactionBoundary, TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY } from '../../domain/message-templates/template-transaction-boundary';
import { PrismaService } from '../../infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { MessageTemplatesController } from './message-templates.controller';
import { TEMPLATE_OBSERVATION_CLOCK, TemplateObservationService } from '../../domain/message-templates/template-observation.service';

/**
 * Superfície BFF (ADR-0006): o que as telas consomem, autenticado por sessão.
 *
 * O middleware é montado no PREFIXO. É isso que faz o guard ser da superfície e
 * não da rota, e é o que impede o defeito que já mordeu os dois sistemas: no
 * Hub, um router.use(requireAuth) sem path interceptou o webhook público.
 */
@Module({
  imports: [ChannelTransportModule, TemplateManagementModule],
  controllers: [
    AuthController,
    MeController,
    BusinessUnitsController,
    ChannelsController,
    NumberRegistrationsController,
    ConversationsController,
    IntegracaoController,
    HistoricoController,
    UsuariosController,
    SetoresController,
    AtividadesController,
    OnboardingController,
    PainelController,
    ArquivosController,
    MessageTemplatesController,
  ],
  providers: [
    SessionService,
    IdentityService,
    PasswordService,
    LoginAttemptService,
    AccessService,
    TemplateDraftService,
    TemplateSubmissionService,
    TemplateObservationService,
    { provide: TEMPLATE_OBSERVATION_CLOCK, useValue: { now: () => new Date() } },
    { provide: TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY, useFactory: (prisma: PrismaService) => new PrismaTemplateTransactionBoundary(prisma), inject: [PrismaService] },
    ChannelsService,
    NumberRegistrationService,
    ConversationsService,
    HistoricoService,
    UsuariosService,
    ApiKeyService,
    TenancyService,
    ModulosService,
    SetorService,
    RegistroDeAtividade,
    AtividadesService,
    OnboardingService,
    PainelService,
    { provide: ArmazenamentoDeArquivo, useClass: ArmazenamentoEmDisco },
    { provide: EnvioDeEmail, useClass: ResendEmail },
    TokensService,
    ConvitesService,
    LimiteDeRecuperacao,
    // Adaptadores de SAÍDA por porta (ADR-0006, Emenda 1): o domínio depende da
    // interface, não do HTTP. É o que deixa o teste provar permissão sem a Meta
    // nem a Evolution no ar.
    { provide: TYPEBOT_CLIENT, useClass: TypebotHttpClient },
  ],
  exports: [SessionService, PasswordService],
})
export class BffModule implements NestModule {
  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer): void {
    consumer
      .apply(SessionMiddleware)
      .forRoutes({ path: 'api/bff/{*path}', method: RequestMethod.ALL });
  }
}
