  const loadCatalogPage = useCallback(async (after: string | null, refresh: boolean, epoch = contextEpoch.current,
    inheritedIntent?: ObservationIntent) => {
    if (!buId || !channelId) return;
    const flightKey = refresh ? `${epoch}:${buId}:${channelId}:${after ?? ''}` : null;
    const existingFlight = flightKey ? catalogRefreshFlights.current.get(flightKey) : undefined;
    if (existingFlight) return existingFlight;
    const intent = refresh ? observationAuthority.beginIntent() : inheritedIntent;
    const passiveRead = intent ? null : observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
    const busyRequest = refresh ? ++catalogBusyRequest.current : null;
    if (refresh) setCatalogBusy(true);
    const canApply = () => epoch === contextEpoch.current && (intent
      ? observationAuthority.canApplyIntent(intent) : observationAuthority.canApplyPassiveRead(passiveRead!));
    const flight = (async () => {
      try {
        const result = refresh
          ? await templateApi.refreshCatalog(buId, channelId, after)
          : await templateApi.catalog(buId, channelId, after);
        if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current) return;
        if ('phase' in result) {
          if (refresh && intent && observationAuthority.canApplyIntent(intent)) {
            setNotice('Outra atualização do catálogo está em andamento. O estado observado anteriormente foi mantido.');
          }
          return;
        }
        if (refresh) {
          if (!intent || !observationAuthority.completeMeaningfulIntent(intent)) return;
        } else if (!canApply()) return;
        const key = after ?? '';
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'catalog-page', key, result, replace: refresh && after === null });
        if (refresh && after === null && result.available) setCatalogFailure(false);
        setCatalogCursor(result.nextCursor);
        if (refresh) setNotice(result.available ? 'Catálogo atualizado.' : 'Catálogo indisponível; os dados observados permanecem identificados como indisponíveis.');
      } catch (cause) {
        ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
        const admittedFailure = epoch === contextEpoch.current && (refresh
          ? Boolean(intent && observationAuthority.completeMeaningfulIntent(intent)) : canApply());
        if (admittedFailure) {
          setCatalogFailure(true);
          setError('Não foi possível consultar o catálogo desta conta.');
        }
      } finally {
        if (refresh && catalogBusyRequest.current === busyRequest) setCatalogBusy(false);
      }
    })();
    if (!flightKey) return flight;
    catalogRefreshFlights.current.set(flightKey, flight);
    try { await flight; }
    finally { if (catalogRefreshFlights.current.get(flightKey) === flight) catalogRefreshFlights.current.delete(flightKey); }
  }, [buId, channelId, observationAuthority]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void api.get<{ businessUnits: BusinessUnit[] }>('/business-units').then((result) => {
      if (!active) return;
      const scoped = result.businessUnits.filter((unit) => unit.channels.some((item) => item.type === 'OFICIAL'));
      setUnits(result.businessUnits);
      const first = scoped[0];
      if (first) {
        setBuId(first.id);
        setChannelId(first.channels.find((item) => item.type === 'OFICIAL')?.id ?? '');
      }
    }).catch((cause: unknown) => { ignorarSessaoMorta(cause); if (active) setError('Não foi possível carregar suas Business Units.'); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!buId || !channelId) { setCapability(null); return; }
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    setCapability(null);
    void templateApi.capabilities(buId, channelId).then((result) => {
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current) setCapability(result);
    }).catch((cause: unknown) => {
      ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current) setCapability({ available: false, expectedGeneration: channel?.generation ?? 0, explanation: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    });
    void loadDraftList(buId, view, page, limit, epoch);
    dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-all' }); setCatalogCursor(null); setCatalogFailure(false);
    void loadCatalogPage(null, false, epoch);
  }, [buId, channelId]);

  useEffect(() => { void loadDraftList(); }, [buId, view, page, limit, loadDraftList]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!buId || !channelId || !capability?.available || !definitionReady) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    const editing = editEpoch.current;
    const requestId = ++previewRequest.current;
    const selectedDraftId = draft?.id;
    const selectedRevision = draft?.revision;
    const editor = editorEpoch.current;
    const definition = structuredClone(form);
    setPreview(null); setPreviewText('');
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      void templateApi.preview(buId, {
        channelId, expectedGeneration: capability.expectedGeneration,
        ...(selectedDraftId ? { draftId: selectedDraftId, expectedRevision: selectedRevision } : {}),
        definition,
      }).then((result) => {
        if (requestId !== previewRequest.current || epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current || editing !== editEpoch.current) return;
        setPreview(result); setPreviewText(result.renderedText); setError(null);
      }).catch((cause: unknown) => {
        if (requestId !== previewRequest.current || epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editing !== editEpoch.current) return;
        if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.status < 500) setError('Revise nome, texto e exemplos antes de pré-visualizar.');
        else setError('Não foi possível gerar a prévia neste momento.');
      });
    }, 300);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [buId, channelId, capability?.available, capability?.expectedGeneration, draft?.id, draft?.revision, definitionReady, form]);

  function trocarBu(nextBuId: string) {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('Descartar as alterações que ainda não foram salvas ao trocar de Business Unit?')) return;
    contextEpoch.current += 1; editorEpoch.current += 1; editEpoch.current += 1;
    listRequest.current += 1; openRequest.current += 1; previewRequest.current += 1; statusRequest.current += 1; operationRequest.current += 1; recoveryRequest.current += 1;
    observationAuthority.reset();
    submitIntent.current = null;
    setRecoveryReference(null);
    setBusy(false);
    catalogBusyRequest.current += 1; setCatalogBusy(false);
    setBuId(nextBuId);
    const next = units.find((unit) => unit.id === nextBuId);
    setChannelId(next?.channels.find((item) => item.type === 'OFICIAL')?.id ?? '');
    setDraft(null); setSavedDefinition(null); setForm(EMPTY); setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); setNotice(null); setError(null);
    setView('mine');
    setPage(1); dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-all' }); setCatalogCursor(null); setCatalogFailure(false);
  }

  function trocarCanal(nextChannelId: string) {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('Descartar as alterações que ainda não foram salvas ao trocar de canal?')) return;
    contextEpoch.current += 1; editorEpoch.current += 1; editEpoch.current += 1;
    openRequest.current += 1; previewRequest.current += 1; statusRequest.current += 1; operationRequest.current += 1; recoveryRequest.current += 1;
    observationAuthority.reset();
    submitIntent.current = null;
    setRecoveryReference(null);
    setBusy(false);
    catalogBusyRequest.current += 1; setCatalogBusy(false);
    setChannelId(nextChannelId); setDraft(null); setSavedDefinition(null); setForm(EMPTY); setPreview(null); setPreviewText('');
    dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-all' }); setCatalogCursor(null); setCatalogFailure(false); setNotice(null); setError(null);
  }

  function editar<K extends keyof TemplateDefinition>(key: K, value: TemplateDefinition[K]) {
    editEpoch.current += 1; setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); setNotice(null); setError(null);
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  }

  async function abrirRascunho(id: string) {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('Descartar as alterações que ainda não foram salvas?')) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    const editor = ++editorEpoch.current;
    const requestId = ++openRequest.current;
    const observationRead = observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
    submitIntent.current = null;
    recoveryRequest.current += 1; operationRequest.current += 1; statusRequest.current += 1;
    previewRequest.current += 1;
    setRecoveryReference(null); dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-individual' }); setPreview(null); setPreviewText('');
    setBusy(true);
    editEpoch.current += 1; previewRequest.current += 1;
    try {
      const opened = await templateApi.getDraft(id);
      if (requestId !== openRequest.current || epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
      if (opened.buId !== buId) return;
      if (opened.channelId !== channelId) {
        contextEpoch.current += 1;
        observationAuthority.reset();
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-all' }); setCatalogCursor(null); setCatalogFailure(false);
      }
      const openedObservationRead = opened.channelId === channelId ? observationRead : observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
      const selectedOperation = operationFromDraft(opened);
      dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: selectedOperation?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
      restoreSavedObservation(opened.savedObservation ?? opened.currentOperation?.savedObservation, openedObservationRead,
        selectedOperation?.remoteTemplateId ?? null);
      setDraft(opened); setSavedDefinition(opened.definition); setForm(opened.definition);
      setChannelId(opened.channelId); setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); setNotice('Rascunho aberto.'); setError(null);
      editEpoch.current += 1;
    } catch (cause) { ignorarSessaoMorta(cause); if (requestId === openRequest.current && epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setError('Não foi possível abrir este rascunho.'); }
    finally { if (requestId === openRequest.current && epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  function createVariationFromDefinition(definition: TemplateDefinition) {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('Descartar as alterações que ainda não foram salvas e copiar este modelo?')) return;
    const stem = definition.name.slice(0, 124).replace(/_+$/g, '');
    const variation = { ...definition, name: `${stem}_v2` };
    contextEpoch.current += 1; editorEpoch.current += 1; editEpoch.current += 1;
    observationAuthority.reset();
    openRequest.current += 1; previewRequest.current += 1;
    submitIntent.current = null;
    setRecoveryReference(null); dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-individual' });
    setDraft(null); setSavedDefinition(null); setForm(variation); setPreview(null); setPreviewText('');
    setBusy(false);
    setNotice('Variação em novo rascunho. O modelo já enviado não será alterado.'); setError(null);
  }

  function createVariation() {
    if (draft) createVariationFromDefinition(draft.definition);
  }

  function novoRascunho() {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('Descartar as alterações que ainda não foram salvas?')) return;
    editorEpoch.current += 1; editEpoch.current += 1; openRequest.current += 1; previewRequest.current += 1;
    submitIntent.current = null; setRecoveryReference(null); dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'clear-individual' });
    setDraft(null); setSavedDefinition(null); setForm(EMPTY); setPreview(null); setPreviewText('');
    setBusy(false);
    setNotice(null); setError(null);
  }

  async function salvar() {
    if (!buId || !channelId || !capability?.available || !definitionReady || isLocked) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    const editor = editorEpoch.current;
    const editing = editEpoch.current;
    const definition = structuredClone(form);
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null);
    try {
      if (draft) {
        const updated = await templateApi.saveDraft(draft.id, draft.revision, definition);
        if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: operationFromDraft(updated)?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
        setDraft(updated); setSavedDefinition(updated.definition);
        if (editing === editEpoch.current) setForm(updated.definition);
        setNotice(`Rascunho salvo na revisão ${updated.revision}.`);
        await loadDraftList(buId, view, page, limit, epoch);
      } else {
        const contextKey = `${buId}:${channelId}`;
        const pending = pendingCreates.current.get(contextKey);
        if (pending && (pending.buId !== buId || pending.channelId !== channelId || pending.generation !== capability.expectedGeneration
          || JSON.stringify(pending.definition) !== JSON.stringify(definition))) {
          setError('Há uma criação ainda não confirmada. Retome exatamente o conteúdo anterior antes de editar ou criar outro rascunho.');
          return;
        }
        const request = pending ?? { key: crypto.randomUUID(), definition, buId, channelId, generation: capability.expectedGeneration };
        pendingCreates.current.set(contextKey, request);
        const created = await templateApi.createDraft(buId, {
          clientRequestId: request.key, channelId, expectedGeneration: capability.expectedGeneration, ...request.definition,
        });
        pendingCreates.current.delete(contextKey);
        if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: operationFromDraft(created)?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
        setDraft(created); setSavedDefinition(created.definition);
        if (editing === editEpoch.current) setForm(created.definition);
        setNotice(`Rascunho salvo na revisão ${created.revision}.`);
        await loadDraftList(buId, view, page, limit, epoch);
      }
    } catch (cause) {
      ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && editing === editEpoch.current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível confirmar o salvamento. O texto continua nesta tela.');
    } finally { if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  async function retomarCriacaoPendente() {
    if (!pendingCreate || pendingCreate.buId !== buId || pendingCreate.channelId !== channelId) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current; const editor = editorEpoch.current;
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null);
    try {
      const created = await templateApi.createDraft(pendingCreate.buId, {
        clientRequestId: pendingCreate.key, channelId: pendingCreate.channelId,
        expectedGeneration: pendingCreate.generation, ...pendingCreate.definition,
      });
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
      pendingCreates.current.delete(`${pendingCreate.buId}:${pendingCreate.channelId}`);
      dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: operationFromDraft(created)?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
      setDraft(created); setSavedDefinition(created.definition);
      setNotice(`Rascunho salvo na revisão ${created.revision}. O texto editado continua nesta tela.`);
      await loadDraftList(buId, view, page, limit, epoch);
    } catch (cause) {
      ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) {
        setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? cause.message : 'Não foi possível confirmar a criação. O mesmo pedido continua disponível para retomada.');
      }
    } finally { if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  async function carregarPrevia() {
    if (!buId || !channelId || !capability?.available || !definitionReady) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current; const editor = editorEpoch.current; const editing = editEpoch.current; const requestId = ++previewRequest.current;
    try {
      const result = await templateApi.preview(buId, { channelId, expectedGeneration: capability.expectedGeneration,
        ...(draft ? { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: draft.revision } : {}), definition: structuredClone(form) });
      if (requestId !== previewRequest.current || epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current || editing !== editEpoch.current) return;
      setPreview(result); setPreviewText(result.renderedText); setError(null);
    } catch (cause) { ignorarSessaoMorta(cause); if (requestId === previewRequest.current && epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && editing === editEpoch.current) setError('Não foi possível gerar a prévia. Revise o conteúdo e tente novamente.'); }
  }

  async function enviar() {
    if (!draft || !canSubmit || !preview?.requestFingerprint || !capability) return;
    const intent = submitIntent.current;
    const key = intent && intent.draftId === draft.id && intent.revision === draft.revision && intent.fingerprint === preview.requestFingerprint
      ? intent.key : crypto.randomUUID();
    submitIntent.current = { key, draftId: draft.id, revision: draft.revision, fingerprint: preview.requestFingerprint };
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    const editor = editorEpoch.current;
    const editing = editEpoch.current;
    const targetDraftId = draft.id;
    const submitObservationRead = observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null);
    try {
      const operation = await templateApi.submit(draft.id, key, { expectedRevision: draft.revision,
        expectedGeneration: capability.expectedGeneration, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: preview.requestFingerprint });
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
      setRecoveryReference(null);
      dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: operation.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
      setDraft((current) => current ? { ...current, state: ['ACCEPTED', 'SENDING', 'PREPARED', 'AMBIGUOUS'].includes(operation.phase) ? 'LOCKED' : current.state,
        currentOperation: operation, recentOperations: [operation, ...(current.recentOperations ?? []).filter((item) => item.id !== operation.id)].slice(0, 11) } : current);
      restoreSavedObservation(operation.savedObservation, submitObservationRead, operation.remoteTemplateId);
      setNotice(operationNotice(operation));
      await loadDraftList(buId, view, page, limit, epoch);
    } catch (cause) {
      ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
      if (cause instanceof ErroDaApi && cause.operationId) {
        if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setRecoveryReference({ operationId: cause.operationId, phase: cause.phase, outcome: cause.outcome,
          acceptance: cause.providerAcceptance, receipt: cause.receiptPersistence });
        try {
          if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
          await templateApi.operation(targetDraftId, cause.operationId);
          if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
          const draftRead = observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
          const authoritative = await templateApi.getDraft(targetDraftId);
          if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) {
            const latestOperation = operationFromDraft(authoritative);
            dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: latestOperation?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
            setDraft((current) => current ? { ...authoritative,
              definition: editEpoch.current === editing ? authoritative.definition : current.definition } : current);
            if (authoritative.revision === draft.revision && latestOperation?.id === cause.operationId) {
              restoreSavedObservation(authoritative.savedObservation ?? latestOperation.savedObservation, draftRead,
                latestOperation.remoteTemplateId);
              setRecoveryReference(null);
            }
            if (editEpoch.current === editing) {
              setSavedDefinition(authoritative.definition);
              setForm(authoritative.definition);
              if (latestOperation?.id === cause.operationId && latestOperation.phase === 'REJECTED') {
                setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); setRecoveryReference(null);
              }
            }
          }
        } catch { /* keep the server's safe operation metadata below */ }
      }
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setError(cause instanceof ErroDaApi ? `${cause.message}${cause.operationId ? ' A operação foi preservada e não será reenviada automaticamente.' : ''}` : 'Não foi possível confirmar o cadastro. Consulte a operação antes de tentar novamente.');
    } finally { if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  async function recuperar() {
    const operation = operationFromDraft(draft);
    if (!draft || (!operation && !recoveryReference)) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current;
    const editor = editorEpoch.current;
    const editing = editEpoch.current;
    const targetDraftId = draft.id;
    const sequence = ++recoveryRequest.current;
    setBusy(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const operationId = operation?.id ?? recoveryReference!.operationId;
      if (operation?.recoveryAction === 'RECONCILE') await templateApi.reconcile(draft.id, operationId);
      else await templateApi.operation(draft.id, operationId);
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current || sequence !== recoveryRequest.current) return;
      const draftRead = observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
      const authoritative = await templateApi.getDraft(targetDraftId);
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current || sequence !== recoveryRequest.current) return;
      const latestOperation = operationFromDraft(authoritative);
      dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: latestOperation?.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
      setDraft((current) => current ? { ...authoritative,
        definition: editEpoch.current === editing ? authoritative.definition : current.definition } : current);
      if (editEpoch.current === editing) {
        setSavedDefinition(authoritative.definition);
        setForm(authoritative.definition);
      }
      if (authoritative.revision === draft.revision && latestOperation?.id === operationId) {
        restoreSavedObservation(authoritative.savedObservation ?? latestOperation.savedObservation, draftRead,
          latestOperation.remoteTemplateId);
        setRecoveryReference(null);
        if (editEpoch.current === editing && latestOperation.phase === 'REJECTED') { setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); }
        setNotice(operationNotice(latestOperation));
      }
    } catch (cause) { ignorarSessaoMorta(cause); if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && sequence === recoveryRequest.current) setError('Não foi possível atualizar esta operação. O identificador continua disponível para consulta.'); }
    finally { if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && sequence === recoveryRequest.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  async function consultarOperacao(operationId: string) {
    if (!draft) return;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current; const editor = editorEpoch.current; const targetDraftId = draft.id; const sequence = ++operationRequest.current;
    const observationRead = observationAuthority.capturePassiveRead();
    try {
      const result = await templateApi.operation(targetDraftId, operationId);
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current || sequence !== operationRequest.current) return;
      setDraft((current) => current ? { ...current,
        currentOperation: result.revision === current.revision ? result : current.currentOperation,
        recentOperations: [result, ...(current.recentOperations ?? []).filter((item) => item.id !== result.id)].slice(0, 11) } : current);
      const selectedOperation = operationFromDraft(draft);
      if (result.revision === draft.revision) {
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'selected-operation', remoteTemplateId: result.remoteTemplateId ?? null });
        if (result.id === selectedOperation?.id && result.remoteTemplateId
          && selectedOperation.remoteTemplateId === result.remoteTemplateId) {
          restoreSavedObservation(result.savedObservation, observationRead, result.remoteTemplateId);
        }
      }
      setNotice(`Operação consultada: ${operationLabel(result)}.`);
    } catch (cause) { ignorarSessaoMorta(cause); if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && sequence === operationRequest.current) setError('Esta operação não está disponível para consulta.'); }
  }

  async function atualizarStatus() {
    if (!draft || !currentOperation?.remoteTemplateId) return;
    const targetRemoteTemplateId = currentOperation.remoteTemplateId;
    const epoch = contextEpoch.current; const editor = editorEpoch.current; const targetDraftId = draft.id; const sequence = ++statusRequest.current;
    const intent = observationAuthority.beginIntent();
    setBusy(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const status = await templateApi.refreshDraft(draft.id);
      if (epoch !== contextEpoch.current || editor !== editorEpoch.current) return;
      if ('phase' in status) {
        if (observationAuthority.canApplyIntent(intent)) setNotice('A atualização do status já está em andamento. O estado anterior foi mantido.');
        return;
      }
      if (!observationAuthority.completeMeaningfulIntent(intent)) return;
      applyIndividualObservation(status);
      setNotice(`Status consultado: ${statusPt[status.status] ?? 'Desconhecido'}.`);
      await loadCatalogPage(null, false, epoch, intent);
    } catch (cause) {
      ignorarSessaoMorta(cause);
      if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current
        && observationAuthority.completeMeaningfulIntent(intent)) {
        dispatchObservationSlices({ type: 'unavailable', remoteTemplateId: targetRemoteTemplateId });
        setError('Não foi possível atualizar o status remoto. O estado anterior continua identificado como indisponível.');
      }
    }
    finally { if (epoch === contextEpoch.current && editor === editorEpoch.current && sequence === statusRequest.current) setBusy(false); }
  }

  async function refreshCatalog(after: string | null = null) {
    await loadCatalogPage(after, true, contextEpoch.current);
  }

  const latestReceipt = currentOperation?.receipts?.[0]
    ?? draft?.recentOperations?.find((operation) => operation.id === currentOperation?.id)?.receipts?.[0];

  return <div className="grade">
    <h1 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Modelos de mensagem</h1>
    <p className="suave">Crie e envie um modelo para análise da Meta. Um cadastro aceito ainda pode estar aguardando aprovação.</p>
    {error && <p className="erro" role="alert">{error}</p>}
    {notice && <p className="suave" role="status">{notice}</p>}

    <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Contexto do canal">
      <label>Business Unit
        <select value={buId} onChange={(event) => trocarBu(event.target.value)}>
          {units.filter((unit) => unit.channels.some((item) => item.type === 'OFICIAL')).map((unit) => <option key={unit.id} value={unit.id}>{unit.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>Canal oficial
        <select value={channelId} onChange={(event) => trocarCanal(event.target.value)}>
          {officialChannels.map((item) => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{activeBu?.name} · {item.status}</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      {capability?.available ? <p role="status">Gerenciamento de modelos disponível · geração {capability.expectedGeneration}</p>
        : capability ? <p role="status">Gerenciamento indisponível neste canal. A prévia não usará a Meta.</p>
          : <p role="status">Carregando capacidade do canal…</p>}
    </section>

    <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Rascunhos de modelo">
      <div className="linha">
        <h2>Rascunhos</h2>
        {canManageBu && <label>Exibição
          <select value={view} onChange={(event) => { setView(event.target.value as 'mine' | 'bu'); setPage(1); }}>
            <option value="mine">Meus rascunhos</option><option value="bu">Rascunhos da BU</option>
          </select>
        </label>}
        <label>Por página
          <select value={limit} onChange={(event) => { setLimit(Number(event.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
            <option value={20}>20</option><option value={50}>50</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="button" onClick={novoRascunho}>Novo rascunho</button>
      </div>
      {pendingCreate && JSON.stringify(pendingCreate.definition) !== JSON.stringify(form) && !draft && <p>
        <span>Uma criação anterior pode ter sido confirmada no servidor.</span>{' '}
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void retomarCriacaoPendente()} disabled={busy}>
          Retomar criação anterior sem perder este texto
        </button>
      </p>}
      <p className="suave">{draftPage.total} rascunhos · página {page} · {view === 'mine' ? 'seus rascunhos' : 'compartilhados na BU'}</p>
      <div className="grade">
        {draftPage.items.map((item) => <div className="linha" key={item.id}>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => void abrirRascunho(item.id)}>Abrir {item.definition.name}</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => createVariationFromDefinition(item.definition)}>Copiar como novo rascunho</button>
          <span>{item.state === 'LOCKED' ? 'Revisão congelada' : 'Rascunho editável'} · revisão {item.revision}</span>
          {item.currentOperation && <span>Operação: {operationLabel(item.currentOperation)}</span>}
        </div>)}
        {!draftPage.items.length && <p className="suave">Ainda não há rascunhos nesta lista.</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="linha">
        <button type="button" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.max(1, current - 1))}>Página anterior</button>
        <button type="button" disabled={!draftPage.hasMore} onClick={() => setPage((current) => current + 1)}>Próxima página</button>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Editor de modelo">
      <h2>{draft ? `Editar ${draft.definition.name} · revisão ${draft.revision}` : 'Novo rascunho de modelo'}</h2>
      <label>Nome do modelo
        <input value={form.name} maxLength={128} onChange={(event) => editar('name', event.target.value)} disabled={isLocked} />
      </label>
      <label>Idioma
        <select value={form.language} onChange={(event) => editar('language', event.target.value as 'pt_BR')} disabled={isLocked}>
          <option value="pt_BR">Português (Brasil)</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>Categoria
        <select value={form.category} onChange={(event) => editar('category', event.target.value as TemplateDefinition['category'])} disabled={isLocked}>
          <option value="MARKETING">Marketing · abertura comercial</option>
          <option value="UTILITY">Utilidade · atualização transacional</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>Cabeçalho estático (opcional)
        <input value={form.header ?? ''} maxLength={60} onChange={(event) => editar('header', event.target.value || undefined)} disabled={isLocked} />
      </label>
      <label>Texto do corpo
        <textarea value={form.body} maxLength={1024} rows={5} onChange={(event) => {
          const nextBody = event.target.value;
          const nextNames = variables(nextBody);
          const examples = Object.fromEntries(nextNames.map((name) => [name, form.examples[name] ?? '']));
          editEpoch.current += 1; setPreview(null); setPreviewText(''); setNotice(null); setError(null);
          setForm((current) => ({ ...current, body: nextBody, examples }));
        }} disabled={isLocked} />
      </label>
      <div className="grade" aria-label="Exemplos das variáveis">
        {variableNames.map((name) => <label key={name}>Exemplo para {name}
          <input value={form.examples[name] ?? ''} maxLength={128} onChange={(event) => editar('examples', { ...form.examples, [name]: event.target.value })} disabled={isLocked} />
        </label>)}
      </div>
      <label>Rodapé estático (opcional)
        <input value={form.footer ?? ''} maxLength={60} onChange={(event) => editar('footer', event.target.value || undefined)} disabled={isLocked} />
      </label>
      {dirty && <p role="status">Há alterações não salvas</p>}
      {preview && <div className="grade" aria-label="Prévia do modelo">
        <strong>Prévia renderizada</strong>
        <p style={{ whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' }}>{previewText}</p>
        <p role="status">{preview.persistence === 'PERSISTED' ? 'Prévia vinculada à revisão salva' : 'Prévia de conteúdo não salvo'}</p>
      </div>}
      <div className="linha">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void carregarPrevia()} disabled={!definitionReady || !capability?.available}>Pré-visualizar</button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void salvar()} disabled={!definitionReady || !capability?.available || busy || isLocked || !dirty}>
          {draft ? 'Salvar alterações' : 'Salvar rascunho'}
        </button>
        {draft?.state === 'DRAFT' && <button type="button" onClick={() => void enviar()} disabled={!canSubmit || busy}>Enviar à Meta para aprovação</button>}
        {isLocked && <button type="button" onClick={createVariation}>Criar variação</button>}
      </div>
      {currentOperation && <section className="grade" aria-label="Operação de submissão">
        <h3>Operação: {operationLabel(currentOperation)}</h3>
        <p>{operationNotice(currentOperation)}</p>
        {latestReceipt && <p>Última atualização registrada: {new Date(latestReceipt.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>}
        {currentOperation.remoteTemplateId && <button type="button" onClick={() => void atualizarStatus()} disabled={busy}>Atualizar status do modelo</button>}
        {['PREPARED', 'SENDING', 'AMBIGUOUS'].includes(currentOperation.phase) && <button type="button" onClick={() => void recuperar()} disabled={busy}>Recuperar estado da operação</button>}
        {(draft?.recentOperations ?? []).filter((item) => item.id !== currentOperation.id).slice(0, 10).map((item) => <p key={item.id}>
          Revisão {item.revision} · {phasePt[item.phase] ?? item.phase} · <button type="button" onClick={() => void consultarOperacao(item.id)}>Consultar operação</button>
        </p>)}
      </section>}
      {recoveryReference && !currentOperation && <section className="grade" aria-label="Operação preservada">
        <p>Há uma operação registrada que não pôde ser carregada agora.</p>
        <p>Identificador: {recoveryReference.operationId}</p>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void recuperar()} disabled={busy}>Consultar operação registrada</button>
      </section>}
      {editorObservation && <p role="status">Status do modelo: {statusPt[editorObservation.status] ?? 'Desconhecido'} · {isEligibleNow(editorObservation, editorObservation.available, true) ? 'elegível para uso futuro' : 'Não elegível para uso'}{observationTime(editorObservation.observedAt) && <> · Observado em <time aria-label={`Observado em ${observationTime(editorObservation.observedAt)}`} dateTime={editorObservation.observedAt}>{observationTime(editorObservation.observedAt)}</time></>}</p>}
    </section>

    <section className="cartao grade" aria-label="Modelos da conta">
      <div className="linha"><h2>Modelos da conta</h2><button type="button" onClick={() => void refreshCatalog(null)} disabled={!capability?.available} aria-busy={catalogBusy}>Atualizar modelos da conta</button></div>
      {catalogUnavailable && <p role="status">Catálogo indisponível ou desatualizado; nenhum item foi marcado como elegível.</p>}
      {Object.entries(catalogPages).length === 0 && <p className="suave">O catálogo ainda não foi consultado.</p>}
      <div className="grade">
        {catalogItems.map((item, index) => <article className="linha" key={`${item.remoteTemplateId}:${index}`}>
          <strong>{item.name ?? 'Modelo sem nome observado'}</strong>
          <span>{item.language ?? 'Idioma desconhecido'} · {item.category}</span>
          <span>{statusPt[item.status] ?? 'Desconhecido'}</span>
          <span>{isEligibleNow(item, catalogPages[Object.keys(catalogPages).find((key) => catalogPages[key].items.includes(item)) ?? '']?.available) ? 'Elegível para uso futuro' : 'Não elegível para uso'}</span>
          {observationTime(item.observedAt) && <span>Observado em <time aria-label={`Observado em ${observationTime(item.observedAt)}`} dateTime={item.observedAt}>{observationTime(item.observedAt)}</time></span>}
          {!item.supported && <span>Formato sem suporte para prévia segura</span>}
        </article>)}
      </div>
      {catalogCursor && <button type="button" onClick={() => void refreshCatalog(catalogCursor)} disabled={catalogBusy}>Carregar próxima página</button>}
      {catalogItems.length === 0 && Object.values(catalogPages).some((item) => item.available) && <p className="suave">Nenhum modelo encontrado nesta página.</p>}
    </section>
  </div>;
}

```

        "relatedQueries": [
          "\n        WITH page_drafts AS MATERIALIZED (\n          SELECT d.id, d.mantenedora_id, d.bu_id, d.creator_user_id, d.channel_id, d.revision,\n            d.state, d.definition, d.created_at, d.updated_at\n          FROM message_template_drafts d\n          WHERE d.bu_id = $1 AND d.creator_user_id = $2\n          ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\n          LIMIT $3 OFFSET $4\n        )\n        SELECT d.id, d.mantenedora_id AS \"mantenedoraId\", d.bu_id AS \"buId\",\n          d.creator_user_id AS \"creatorUserId\", d.channel_id AS \"channelId\", d.revision,\n          d.state::text AS state, d.definition, d.created_at AS \"createdAt\", d.updated_at AS \"updatedAt\",\n          current_op.id AS \"operationId\", current_op.phase::text AS \"operationPhase\",\n          current_op.revision AS \"operationRevision\", current_op.safe_code AS \"operationSafeCode\",\n          current_op.remote_template_id AS \"operationRemoteTemplateId\",\n          current_op.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"operationAcceptanceProvenance\",\n          current_op.acknowledged_remote_template_id AS \"operationAcknowledgedRemoteTemplateId\",\n          current_op.updated_at AS \"operationUpdatedAt\", current_op.lease_until AS \"operationLeaseUntil\",\n          current_op.recovery_lease_until AS \"operationRecoveryLeaseUntil\"\n        FROM page_drafts d\n        LEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n          SELECT op.id, op.phase, op.revision, op.safe_code, op.remote_template_id,\n            op.acceptance_provenance, op.acknowledged_remote_template_id, op.updated_at, op.lease_until, op.recovery_lease_until\n          FROM message_template_operations op\n          WHERE op.draft_id = d.id AND op.bu_id = d.bu_id AND op.mantenedora_id = d.mantenedora_id\n          ORDER BY op.revision DESC LIMIT 1\n        ) current_op ON TRUE\n        ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC",
          "\n        WITH page_drafts AS MATERIALIZED (\n          SELECT d.id, d.mantenedora_id, d.bu_id, d.creator_user_id, d.channel_id, d.revision,\n            d.state, d.definition, d.created_at, d.updated_at\n          FROM message_template_drafts d\n          WHERE d.bu_id = $1 AND d.creator_user_id = $2\n          ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\n          LIMIT $3 OFFSET $4\n        )\n        SELECT d.id, d.mantenedora_id AS \"mantenedoraId\", d.bu_id AS \"buId\",\n          d.creator_user_id AS \"creatorUserId\", d.channel_id AS \"channelId\", d.revision,\n          d.state::text AS state, d.definition, d.created_at AS \"createdAt\", d.updated_at AS \"updatedAt\",\n          current_op.id AS \"operationId\", current_op.phase::text AS \"operationPhase\",\n          current_op.revision AS \"operationRevision\", current_op.safe_code AS \"operationSafeCode\",\n          current_op.remote_template_id AS \"operationRemoteTemplateId\",\n          current_op.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"operationAcceptanceProvenance\",\n          current_op.acknowledged_remote_template_id AS \"operationAcknowledgedRemoteTemplateId\",\n          current_op.updated_at AS \"operationUpdatedAt\", current_op.lease_until AS \"operationLeaseUntil\",\n          current_op.recovery_lease_until AS \"operationRecoveryLeaseUntil\"\n        FROM page_drafts d\n        LEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n          SELECT op.id, op.phase, op.revision, op.safe_code, op.remote_template_id,\n            op.acceptance_provenance, op.acknowledged_remote_template_id, op.updated_at, op.lease_until, op.recovery_lease_until\n          FROM message_template_operations op\n          WHERE op.draft_id = d.id AND op.bu_id = d.bu_id AND op.mantenedora_id = d.mantenedora_id\n          ORDER BY op.revision DESC LIMIT 1\n        ) current_op ON TRUE\n        ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC"
        ],

        "operationQueries": [
          "\n      WITH recent_operations AS (\n        SELECT id, phase, revision, safe_code, remote_template_id, acceptance_provenance, acknowledged_remote_template_id, updated_at,\n          lease_until, recovery_lease_until, mantenedora_id, channel_id, provider_resource_identity_id,\n          account_reference, channel_generation, pairing_epoch, active_credential_version, definition_snapshot\n        FROM message_template_operations\n        WHERE draft_id = $1 AND bu_id = $2 AND mantenedora_id = $3\n        ORDER BY revision DESC LIMIT 11\n      )\n      SELECT op.id AS \"operationId\", op.phase::text AS phase, op.revision, op.safe_code AS \"safeCode\",\n        op.remote_template_id AS \"remoteTemplateId\", op.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"acceptanceProvenance\",\n        op.acknowledged_remote_template_id AS \"acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId\",\n        op.updated_at AS \"updatedAt\", op.lease_until AS \"leaseUntil\", op.recovery_lease_until AS \"recoveryLeaseUntil\",\n        op.mantenedora_id AS \"operationMantenedoraId\", op.channel_id AS \"operationChannelId\",\n        op.provider_resource_identity_id AS \"operationProviderResourceIdentityId\",\n        op.account_reference AS \"operationAccountReference\", op.channel_generation AS \"operationChannelGeneration\",\n        op.pairing_epoch AS \"operationPairingEpoch\", op.active_credential_version AS \"operationActiveCredentialVersion\",\n        op.definition_snapshot AS \"definitionSnapshot\",\n        receipt.id AS \"receiptId\", receipt.sequence AS \"receiptSequence\", receipt.phase::text AS \"receiptPhase\",\n        receipt.safe_code AS \"receiptSafeCode\", receipt.remote_template_id AS \"receiptRemoteTemplateId\",\n        receipt.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"receiptAcceptanceProvenance\", receipt.created_at AS \"receiptCreatedAt\"\n      FROM recent_operations op\n      LEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n        SELECT id, sequence, phase, safe_code, remote_template_id, acceptance_provenance, created_at\n        FROM message_template_submission_receipts\n        WHERE operation_id = op.id\n        ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 10\n      ) receipt ON TRUE\n      ORDER BY op.revision DESC, receipt.sequence DESC"
        ],
        "receiptQueries": [
          "\n      WITH recent_operations AS (\n        SELECT id, phase, revision, safe_code, remote_template_id, acceptance_provenance, acknowledged_remote_template_id, updated_at,\n definition_snapshot\n        FROM message_template_operations\n        WHERE draft_id = $1 AND bu_id = $2 AND mantenedora_id = $3\n        ORDER BY revision DESC LIMIT 11\n      )\n      SELECT op.id AS \"operationId\", op.phase::text AS phase, op.revision, op.safe_code AS \"safeCode\",\n        op.remote_template_id AS \"remoteTemplateId\", op.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"acceptanceProvenance\",\n        op.acknowledged_remote_template_id AS \"acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId\",\n        op.updated_at AS \"updatedAt\", op.lease_until AS \"leaseUntil\", op.recovery_lease_until AS \"recoveryLeaseUntil\",\n        op.mantenedora_id AS \"operationMantenedoraId\", op.channel_id AS \"operationChannelId\",\n        op.provider_resource_identity_id AS \"operationProviderResourceIdentityId\",\n        op.account_reference AS \"operationAccountReference\", op.channel_generation AS \"operationChannelGeneration\",\n        op.pairing_epoch AS \"operationPairingEpoch\", op.active_credential_version AS \"operationActiveCredentialVersion\",\n        op.definition_snapshot AS \"definitionSnapshot\",\n        receipt.id AS \"receiptId\", receipt.sequence AS \"receiptSequence\", receipt.phase::text AS \"receiptPhase\",\n        receipt.safe_code AS \"receiptSafeCode\", receipt.remote_template_id AS \"receiptRemoteTemplateId\",\n        receipt.acceptance_provenance::text AS \"receiptAcceptanceProvenance\", receipt.created_at AS \"receiptCreatedAt\"\n      FROM recent_operations op\n      LEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n        SELECT id, sequence, phase, safe_code, remote_template_id, acceptance_provenance, created_at\n        FROM message_template_submission_receipts\n        WHERE operation_id = op.id\n        ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 10\n      ) receipt ON TRUE\n      ORDER BY op.revision DESC, receipt.sequence DESC"

def repo_root() -> Path:
    """Resolve by repository sentinels, never by a hard-coded parent index."""
    here = Path(__file__).resolve()
    for candidate in here.parents:
        if ((candidate / "AGENTS.md").is_file()
                and (candidate / "api-app/prisma/schema.prisma").is_file()
                and (candidate / "web-app/Dockerfile").is_file()):
            return candidate
    raise RuntimeError("repository root sentinel not found")

def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()


def this_helper_sha256() -> str:
    return sha256_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())


def safe_run(args: list[str], *, input_text: str | None = None, env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
             timeout: int = 120, cwd: Path | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    # Callers must never relay stdout/stderr from Docker/Prisma on failure.
    return subprocess.run(args, input=input_text, env=env, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True,
                          text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)

def canonical_head(repo_rel: str) -> str:
    # Git for Windows receives only checkout-relative paths; execute from root.
    result = safe_run(["git.exe", "-C", repo_rel, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], timeout=15, cwd=ROOT)
    if result.returncode or result.stderr or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}\n?", result.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError("canonical product HEAD read failed")
    return result.stdout.strip()


def verify_static_sources() -> dict:
    actual_heads = {
        "root": canonical_head("."),
        "api": canonical_head("api-app"),
        "web": canonical_head("web-app"),
    }
    if actual_heads != EXPECTED_HEADS:
        raise RuntimeError("canonical product HEAD differs from accepted freeze")
    freeze_path = ROOT / FREEZE_REL
    if sha256_bytes(freeze_path.read_bytes()) != FREEZE_SHA256:
        raise RuntimeError("accepted source freeze changed")
    freeze = json.loads(freeze_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    entries = {row["path"]: row["sha256"] for row in freeze["records"]}
    if len(entries) != 38:
        raise RuntimeError("source freeze inventory cardinality mismatch")
    for rel, expected in entries.items():
        if sha256_bytes((ROOT / rel).read_bytes()) != expected:
            raise RuntimeError("frozen public source differs")

    inherited = json.loads((ROOT / INHERITED_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    protected = [r for r in inherited["records"] if not r["active_mutation_admitted"]]
    if len(protected) != 30:
        raise RuntimeError("protected original inventory cardinality mismatch")
    for row in protected:
        repo_prefix = {"root": "", "api": "api-app/", "web": "web-app/"}[row["repository"]]
        rel = repo_prefix + row["path"]
        if sha256_bytes((ROOT / rel).read_bytes()) != row["sha256"]:
            raise RuntimeError("protected original source changed")

    actual_names = sorted(p.name for p in (ROOT / "api-app/prisma/migrations").iterdir()
                          if p.is_dir() and not p.is_symlink())
    if actual_names != sorted(MIGRATIONS) or len(actual_names) != 25:
        raise RuntimeError("Prisma migration inventory mismatch")
    for name, expected in MIGRATIONS.items():
        source = ROOT / "api-app/prisma/migrations" / name / "migration.sql"
        sql = source.read_bytes()
        if sha256_bytes(sql) != expected:
            raise RuntimeError("approved migration SQL differs")
        if name in PENDING and not all(token in sql for token in (
                b"BEGIN;", b"SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';",
                b"SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '60s';", b"COMMIT;")):
            raise RuntimeError("approved migration lost transactional timeout guards")
    return {"freeze_sha256": FREEZE_SHA256, "source_count": 38,
            "protected_original_count": 30, "migration_count": 25, "heads_verified": True}


def ids_for_service(service: str) -> list[str]:
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "ps", "--all", "--quiet", "--no-trunc",
                       "--filter", f"label=com.docker.compose.project={PROJECT}",
                       "--filter", f"label=com.docker.compose.service={service}"])
    if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("labeled container lookup failed")
    return [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]


def inspect_projection(container_id: str, service: str) -> dict:
    template = (
        '{{printf "{\\"id\\":%q,\\"status\\":%q,\\"project\\":%q,\\"service\\":%q,\\"owner\\":%q,\\"network_aliases\\":%s}" '
        '.Id .State.Status (index .Config.Labels "com.docker.compose.project") '
        '(index .Config.Labels "com.docker.compose.service") '
        '(index .Config.Labels "com.leadshug.channels.owner") '
        '(json (index .NetworkSettings.Networks "leadshug_channels_local_default").Aliases)}}'
    )
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "inspect", "--format", template, container_id], timeout=20)
    if result.returncode or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("bounded container metadata projection failed")
    row = json.loads(result.stdout)
    if (row.get("id") != container_id or row.get("status") != "running"
            or row.get("project") != PROJECT or row.get("service") != service
            or row.get("owner") != OWNER_LABEL):
        raise RuntimeError("container project/service/owner/state mismatch")
    if not isinstance(row.get("network_aliases"), list):
        raise RuntimeError("expected project network attachment missing")
    return row


def api_database_url_in_memory(container_id: str) -> str:
    # Docker's Go template filters to exactly one Config.Env key before stdout;
    # no peer environment, dotenv content, or other API variables are returned.
    template = '{{range .Config.Env}}{{if eq (index (split . "=") 0) "DATABASE_URL"}}{{.}}{{end}}{{end}}'
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "inspect", "--format", template, container_id], timeout=20)
    if result.returncode or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("exact API database setting projection failed")
    line = result.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")
    if not line.startswith("DATABASE_URL=") or "\n" in line or "\r" in line:
        raise RuntimeError("exact API database setting is unavailable")
    return line.partition("=")[2]

def topology_snapshot() -> tuple[dict, dict, dict]:
    target_ids = {}
    target_rows = {}
    for service in ("api", "postgres"):
        ids = ids_for_service(service)
        if len(ids) != 1 or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", ids[0]):
            raise RuntimeError("target service full ID is not unique")
        target_ids[service] = ids[0]
        target_rows[service] = inspect_projection(ids[0], service)

    preserved_ids = {}
    for service in PRESERVED_SERVICES:
        ids = ids_for_service(service)
        if len(ids) != 1 or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", ids[0]):
            raise RuntimeError("preserved service full ID is not unique")
        row = inspect_projection(ids[0], service)
        preserved_ids[service] = row["id"]

    network_result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "network", "inspect", "--format", '{{printf "%s|%s" .Id .Name}}', NETWORK], timeout=20)
    if network_result.returncode or network_result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("project network metadata projection failed")
    network_fields = network_result.stdout.rstrip("\r\n").split("|", 1)
    if len(network_fields) != 2 or network_fields[1] != NETWORK or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", network_fields[0]):
        raise RuntimeError("project network identity mismatch")

    volume_names = set()
    for volume in PRESERVED_VOLUMES:
        volume_result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "volume", "inspect", "--format", "{{.Name}}", volume], timeout=20)
        if volume_result.returncode or volume_result.stderr:
            raise RuntimeError("preserved volume metadata projection failed")
        volume_names.add(volume_result.stdout.strip())
    if volume_names != set(PRESERVED_VOLUMES):
        raise RuntimeError("preserved volume inventory mismatch")

    pg_aliases = target_rows["postgres"]["network_aliases"] or []
    if "postgres" not in pg_aliases:
        raise RuntimeError("PostgreSQL service alias mismatch")

    api_url = api_database_url_in_memory(target_ids["api"])
    url = urlsplit(api_url)
    db_name = unquote(url.path.lstrip("/"))
    if (url.scheme not in ("postgres", "postgresql") or url.hostname != "postgres"
            or (url.port or 5432) != 5432 or db_name != "leadshug_channels_local"):
        raise RuntimeError("in-memory API DB endpoint does not match owned PostgreSQL service")
    # current_database() is checked by the one read-only catalog transaction.
    safe_snapshot = {
        "api_id": target_ids["api"], "postgres_id": target_ids["postgres"],
        "preserved_ids": preserved_ids, "network_id": network_fields[0],
        "volumes": sorted(PRESERVED_VOLUMES), "db_endpoint_matches_owned_service": True,
    }
    return safe_snapshot, {}, {"DATABASE_URL": api_url}


CATALOG_JS = r'''const {PrismaClient}=require('@prisma/client');
const p=new PrismaClient();
(async()=>{
 const out=await p.$transaction(async tx=>{
  await tx.$executeRawUnsafe('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY');
  await tx.$executeRawUnsafe("SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'");
  await tx.$executeRawUnsafe("SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '60s'");
  const args=process.argv.slice(1).map(JSON.parse);
  const db=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe('SELECT current_database() AS db');
  const migrations=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT migration_name,checksum,
    finished_at IS NOT NULL AS finished,rolled_back_at IS NOT NULL AS rolled_back
    FROM "_prisma_migrations" ORDER BY migration_name`);
  const tables=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT tablename AS name FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname='public' AND tablename=ANY($1::text[]) ORDER BY 1`,args[0]);
  const enums=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT typname AS name FROM pg_type t JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=t.typnamespace WHERE n.nspname='public' AND t.typtype='e' AND typname=ANY($1::text[]) ORDER BY 1`,args[1]);
  const indexes=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT indexname AS name FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname='public' AND indexname=ANY($1::text[]) ORDER BY 1`,args[2]);
  const constraints=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT conname AS name FROM pg_constraint WHERE connamespace='public'::regnamespace AND conname=ANY($1::text[]) ORDER BY 1`,args[3]);
  const functions=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT p.proname AS name FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=p.pronamespace WHERE n.nspname='public' AND p.proname=ANY($1::text[]) GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`,args[4]);
  const triggers=await tx.$queryRawUnsafe(`SELECT tgname AS name FROM pg_trigger WHERE NOT tgisinternal AND tgname=ANY($1::text[]) ORDER BY 1`,args[5]);
  return {db:db[0]?.db,migrations:migrations.map(r=>({name:r.migration_name,checksum:r.checksum,finished:r.finished,rolledBack:r.rolled_back})),tables:tables.map(r=>r.name),enums:enums.map(r=>r.name),indexes:indexes.map(r=>r.name),constraints:constraints.map(r=>r.name),functions:functions.map(r=>r.name),triggers:triggers.map(r=>r.name)};
 },{maxWait:5000,timeout:70000});
 console.log(JSON.stringify(out));
})().catch(async()=>{try{await p.$disconnect()}catch{};process.exitCode=2}).finally(async()=>{try{await p.$disconnect()}catch{}});'''


def catalog_probe(api_id: str) -> dict:
    args_json = [TABLES, ENUMS, INDEXES, CONSTRAINTS, FUNCTIONS, TRIGGERS]
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "exec", api_id, "node", "-e", CATALOG_JS,
                       *(json.dumps(x, separators=(",", ":")) for x in args_json)], timeout=85)
    if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("bounded Prisma metadata query failed")
    value = json.loads(result.stdout)
    if set(value) != {"db", "migrations", "tables", "enums", "indexes", "constraints", "functions", "triggers"}:
        raise RuntimeError("metadata response shape mismatch")
    return value


def verify_catalog(meta: dict, *, applied: bool) -> dict:
    if meta.get("db") != "leadshug_channels_local":
        raise RuntimeError("connected current_database does not match approved TEST target")
    rows = meta["migrations"]
    names = [row["name"] for row in rows]
    expected_names = set(MIGRATIONS) if applied else set(MIGRATIONS) - set(PENDING)
    if len(names) != len(expected_names) or len(set(names)) != len(names) or set(names) != expected_names:
        raise RuntimeError("database migration inventory mismatch")
    for row in rows:
        name = row["name"]
        if row["checksum"] != MIGRATIONS[name] or row["rolledBack"]:
            raise RuntimeError("database migration checksum/rollback mismatch")
        if row["finished"] is not True:
            raise RuntimeError("database migration completion state mismatch")
    expected_objects = {"tables": TABLES, "enums": ENUMS, "indexes": INDEXES,
                        "constraints": CONSTRAINTS, "functions": FUNCTIONS, "triggers": TRIGGERS}
    for kind, expected in expected_objects.items():
        actual = meta[kind]
        if applied and actual != sorted(expected):
            raise RuntimeError("post-migration catalog object mismatch")
        if not applied and actual:
            raise RuntimeError("partial template catalog exists before migration")
    return {"migration_count": 25, "completed_count": 25 if applied else 23,
            "pending": [] if applied else PENDING, "template_catalog_complete": applied}


def read_go(path: Path, expected_sha: str, phase: str) -> dict:
    if sha256_bytes(path.read_bytes()) != expected_sha:
        raise RuntimeError("parent GO receipt hash mismatch")
    doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if (doc.get("schema") != "stcon05-local-activation-go-v1"
            or doc.get("status") != "approved"
            or doc.get("phase") != phase
            or doc.get("target_project") != PROJECT
            or doc.get("target_database") != "leadshug_channels_local"
            or doc.get("source_freeze_sha256") != FREEZE_SHA256
            or doc.get("migration_names") != PENDING
            or doc.get("helper_sha256") != this_helper_sha256()):
        raise RuntimeError("parent GO receipt scope mismatch")
    return doc


def write_exclusive(path: Path, payload: dict) -> str:
    allowed = (ROOT / ARTIFACT_DIR_REL).resolve()
    resolved = path.resolve()
    if allowed not in resolved.parents:
        raise RuntimeError("receipt path is outside the private preparation directory")
    data = (json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n").encode()
    fd = os.open(resolved, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
        stream.write(data)
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    return sha256_bytes(data)


def docker_compose_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["COMPOSE_DISABLE_ENV_FILE"] = "1"
    forwarded = [item.split("/", 1)[0] for item in env.get("WSLENV", "").split(":") if item]
    if "COMPOSE_DISABLE_ENV_FILE" not in forwarded:
        forwarded.append("COMPOSE_DISABLE_ENV_FILE")
    env["WSLENV"] = ":".join(forwarded)
    return env


def compose_prefix() -> list[str]:
    return ["docker.exe", "compose", "--project-name", PROJECT,
            "--env-file", "NUL", "-f", "-"]


def verify_candidate_image(image: str, image_id: str) -> None:
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._/:@+-]+", image):
        raise RuntimeError("candidate image reference syntax rejected")
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "image", "inspect", "--format", "{{.Id}}", image], timeout=20)
    if result.returncode or result.stderr or result.stdout.strip() != image_id:
        raise RuntimeError("candidate migration image identity mismatch")
    verify_image_contents(image)


def verify_image_contents(image: str) -> None:
    """Inspect only the already-built candidate image, with no network or mounts."""
    js = r'''const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),crypto=require('crypto');
const root='/app/prisma/migrations';const names=fs.readdirSync(root,{withFileTypes:true}).filter(x=>x.isDirectory()).map(x=>x.name).sort();
const expected=JSON.parse(process.argv[1]);const hashes={};for(const n of names){const p=path.join(root,n,'migration.sql');hashes[n]=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(p)).digest('hex')}
const schema=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync('/app/prisma/schema.prisma')).digest('hex');
console.log(JSON.stringify({names,hashes,schema}));'''
    expected_hashes = MIGRATIONS
    result = safe_run(["docker.exe", "run", "--rm", "--network", "none", "--read-only",
                       "--entrypoint", "node", image, "-e", js,
                       json.dumps(expected_hashes, separators=(",", ":"))], timeout=120)
    if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("candidate image source readback failed")
    got = json.loads(result.stdout)
    schema_sha = "fcbeb82ee13f96fd71ec83eb18b29749ba51bfcdd86fe82108d6e8a3b96efd69"
    if (got.get("names") != sorted(MIGRATIONS) or got.get("hashes") != MIGRATIONS
            or got.get("schema") != schema_sha):
        raise RuntimeError("candidate image Prisma source differs from frozen source")


def compose_payload(image: str, db_url: str) -> str:
    fragment = {
        "services": {"migration": {
            "image": image, "pull_policy": "never", "working_dir": "/app",
            "entrypoint": ["/app/node_modules/.bin/prisma"],
            "environment": {"DATABASE_URL": db_url.replace("$", "$$")},
            "networks": {"default": {"aliases": ["stcon05-migration"]}},
            "labels": {"com.leadshug.channels.owner": OWNER_LABEL},
        }},
        "networks": {"default": {"external": True, "name": NETWORK}},
    }
    return json.dumps(fragment, separators=(",", ":"))


def compose_config(payload: str, expected_image: str, db_url: str) -> None:
    result = safe_run(compose_prefix() + ["config", "--format", "json"],
                      input_text=payload, env=docker_compose_env())
    if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr:
        raise RuntimeError("stdin Compose fragment validation failed")
    resolved = json.loads(result.stdout)
    services = resolved.get("services") or {}
    svc = services.get("migration") or {}
    if (set(services) != {"migration"} or svc.get("image") != expected_image
            or svc.get("environment") != {"DATABASE_URL": db_url}
            or svc.get("ports") or svc.get("volumes") or svc.get("env_file")
            or resolved.get("networks", {}).get("default", {}).get("name") != NETWORK):
        raise RuntimeError("resolved migration service differs from bounded fragment")


def compose_once(payload: str, args: list[str], env: dict[str, str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    # `--rm` owns and removes only this uniquely named, one-off migration service.
    return safe_run(compose_prefix() + ["run", "--no-deps", "-T", "--rm", *args],
                    input_text=payload, env=env, timeout=1800)


def run_preflight(go: dict, output: Path) -> None:
    sources = verify_static_sources()
    topology, api, private_env = topology_snapshot()
    api_id = topology["api_id"]
    before = catalog_probe(api_id)
    expected = verify_catalog(before, applied=False)
    receipt = {
        "schema": "stcon05-metadata-preflight-v1",
        "created_at_utc": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
        "authorization_sha256": go["_sha256"],
        "helper_sha256": this_helper_sha256(),
        "source": sources,
        "topology": topology,
        "catalog": expected,
        "database_target_verified_in_memory": True,
        "configuration_values_persisted": False,
        "mutation_performed": False,
    }
    # Keep explicit references short-lived; never stringify or include Config.Env.
    private_env.clear()
    api.clear()
    receipt_sha = write_exclusive(output, receipt)
    print(json.dumps({"phase": "preflight", "receipt": str(output),
                      "receipt_sha256": receipt_sha, "migration_count": 25,
                      "completed": 23, "pending": 2, "mutation_performed": False}, sort_keys=True))


def run_apply(go: dict, go_sha: str, prior_path: Path, prior_sha: str,
              image: str, image_id: str, result_path: Path) -> None:
    if sha256_bytes(prior_path.read_bytes()) != prior_sha:
        raise RuntimeError("preflight receipt hash mismatch")
    prior = json.loads(prior_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if (prior.get("schema") != "stcon05-metadata-preflight-v1"
            or prior.get("helper_sha256") != this_helper_sha256()
            or prior.get("authorization_sha256") != go.get("preflight_authorization_sha256")
            or prior.get("source", {}).get("freeze_sha256") != FREEZE_SHA256
            or prior.get("catalog", {}).get("pending") != PENDING
            or prior.get("topology", {}).get("db_endpoint_matches_owned_service") is not True
            or go.get("preflight_receipt_sha256") != prior_sha
            or go.get("candidate_api_image") != image
            or go.get("candidate_api_image_id") != image_id):
        raise RuntimeError("preflight receipt is not an exact approved baseline")

    sources = verify_static_sources()
    topology, api, private_env = topology_snapshot()
    if topology != prior["topology"]:
        raise RuntimeError("runtime preservation snapshot changed since preflight")
    before = catalog_probe(topology["api_id"])
    verify_catalog(before, applied=False)
    verify_candidate_image(image, image_id)
    payload = compose_payload(image, private_env["DATABASE_URL"])
    compose_config(payload, image, private_env["DATABASE_URL"])

    marker = (ROOT / ARTIFACT_DIR_REL / "apply-once.r41.lock").resolve()
    try:
        fd = os.open(marker, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
    except FileExistsError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("one-time apply marker already exists; no retry") from exc
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as lock:
        lock.write(json.dumps({"authorization_sha256": go_sha,
                               "started_at_utc": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())}))
        lock.flush()
        os.fsync(lock.fileno())

    env = docker_compose_env()
    deploy = compose_once(payload, ["migration", "migrate", "deploy"], env)
    if deploy.returncode != 0:
        private_env.clear()
        api.clear()
        # Deliberately no deploy output, status repair, retry, resolve or rollback.
        write_exclusive(result_path, {"schema": "stcon05-apply-result-v1", "status": "uncertain-failure",
                                      "deploy_exit": deploy.returncode, "retry": False,
                                      "repair_or_resolve": False})
        raise RuntimeError("single migration deploy did not complete; stop for review")

    status = compose_once(payload, ["migration", "migrate", "status"], env)
    after = catalog_probe(topology["api_id"])
    expected = verify_catalog(after, applied=True)
    if status.returncode != 0:
        private_env.clear()
        api.clear()
        write_exclusive(result_path, {"schema": "stcon05-apply-result-v1", "status": "post-status-failure",
                                      "deploy_exit": 0, "status_exit": status.returncode,
                                      "catalog_verified": True, "retry": False})
        raise RuntimeError("deploy succeeded but migrate status failed; stop for review")
    topology_after, api_after, secret_after = topology_snapshot()
    if topology_after != topology:
        raise RuntimeError("non-target topology changed across migration")
    result = {
        "schema": "stcon05-apply-result-v1", "status": "verified",
        "authorization_sha256": go_sha, "preflight_sha256": prior_sha,
        "source": sources, "topology_preserved": True,
        "catalog": expected, "deploy_exit": 0, "migrate_status_exit": 0,
        "retry": False, "configuration_values_persisted": False,
    }
    private_env.clear()
    secret_after.clear()
    api.clear()
    api_after.clear()
    write_exclusive(result_path, result)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--phase", choices=("preflight", "apply"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--go-json", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--go-sha256", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--receipt-out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--preflight-json", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--preflight-sha256")
    parser.add_argument("--candidate-image")
    parser.add_argument("--candidate-image-id")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        verify_static_sources()
        go = read_go(args.go_json, args.go_sha256, args.phase)
        go["_sha256"] = args.go_sha256
        output = args.receipt_out.resolve()
        if args.phase == "preflight":
            run_preflight(go, output)
        else:
            if not all((args.preflight_json, args.preflight_sha256,
                        args.candidate_image, args.candidate_image_id)):
                raise RuntimeError("apply phase requires prior preflight and exact candidate image identity")
            run_apply(go, args.go_sha256, args.preflight_json, args.preflight_sha256,
                      args.candidate_image, args.candidate_image_id, output)
        return 0
    except Exception:
        # Never print exception strings, subprocess output, DB settings, or URL material.
        print(json.dumps({"status": "stopped", "reason": "fail-closed validation gate"}), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

## Embedded file: foundation_documentation/artifacts/tmp/stcon05-access-r41-v1.1/launch-owned-quick-tunnel-r41-v1.1.ps1
SHA256 775680c81f6d0077bae019bb474ecd2407bf4d2f927e18fe6c85f0f38f70b9bc

```
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [switch]$LaunchNewSupervisor,
    [string]$AcceptedProofPath
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$sourceDir = 'C:\unifast\leadshug\leadshug_now_docker\foundation_documentation\artifacts\tmp\number-onboarding-execution\T\cloudflared-cp64'
$runtimeDir = 'C:\unifast\leadshug\leadshug_now_docker\foundation_documentation\artifacts\tmp\stcon05-access-r41-v1.1\cloudflared-runtime'
$sourceSupervisor = Join-Path $sourceDir 'cp64-supervisor.exe'
$sourceCloudflared = Join-Path $sourceDir 'cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe'
$sourceEmptyConfig = Join-Path $sourceDir 'empty-config.yml'
$st06Dir = 'C:\unifast\leadshug\leadshug_now_docker\foundation_documentation\artifacts\tmp\stcon06-executor-v13r4-activation\cloudflared-runtime'
$st06SupervisorPath = Join-Path $st06Dir 'cp64-supervisor.exe'
$st06CloudflaredPath = Join-Path $st06Dir 'cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe'
$launchGoPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'launch-root-go-v1.1.json'
$supervisorPath = Join-Path $runtimeDir 'cp64-supervisor.exe'
$cloudflaredPath = Join-Path $runtimeDir 'cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe'
$emptyConfigPath = Join-Path $runtimeDir 'empty-config.yml'
$statePath = Join-Path $runtimeDir 'runtime-state.json'
$launchReceiptPath = Join-Path $runtimeDir 'launch-receipt.json'
$supervisorSha = 'bdf68028ea2da0699b1e04d191453b5d7090861b17f518d236ce6b084d45c69b'
$cloudflaredSha = '86aee4017b26625cee8484c113558f48effa4cd47f7aa05fcf425604e5d2b23c'
$target = 'http://127.0.0.1:8083'

function Get-Sha256([string]$Path) {
    (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
}

function Get-ExactPathMatches([string]$Path) {
    $slash = [string][char]92
    $escaped = $Path.Replace($slash, $slash + $slash).Replace("'", $slash + "'")
    $query = "SELECT ProcessId, ParentProcessId, ExecutablePath, CreationDate FROM Win32_Process WHERE ExecutablePath = '$escaped'"
    @(Get-CimInstance -Query $query -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object { $_.ExecutablePath -ceq $Path })
}

if (-not $LaunchNewSupervisor) {
    $sourcesMatch = (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sourceSupervisor -PathType Leaf) -and
        (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sourceCloudflared -PathType Leaf) -and
        (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sourceEmptyConfig -PathType Leaf) -and
        ((Get-Sha256 $sourceSupervisor) -ceq $supervisorSha) -and
        ((Get-Sha256 $sourceCloudflared) -ceq $cloudflaredSha) -and
        ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $sourceEmptyConfig).Length -eq 0)
    $runtimePathAbsent = -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $runtimeDir)
    [ordered]@{
        result = 'preparation_check_only_no_process_mutation'
        acceptedSourcesMatch = $sourcesMatch
        freshRuntimePathAbsent = $runtimePathAbsent
        target = $target
        processLaunchAttempted = $false
        boundary = [ordered]@{ processMutation = $false; configOrEnvMutation = $false; processArgvEnumeration = $false }
    } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress
    exit 0
}

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($AcceptedProofPath) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $AcceptedProofPath -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'accepted_go_proof_required' }
if ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($AcceptedProofPath) -cne [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($launchGoPath)) { throw 'root_go_path_mismatch' }
$proof = Get-Content -LiteralPath $launchGoPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$selfSha = Get-Sha256 $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
if ($proof.decision -cne 'GO' -or $proof.operation -cne 'launch_new_quick_tunnel' -or
    $proof.signedBy -cne '/root' -or $proof.executionAuthorityGranted -ne $true -or
    $proof.helperSha256 -cne $selfSha -or $proof.supervisorSha256 -cne $supervisorSha -or
    $proof.cloudflaredSha256 -cne $cloudflaredSha -or $proof.emptyConfigBytes -ne 0 -or
    $proof.target -cne $target -or $proof.runtimeDirectory -cne $runtimeDir) {
    throw 'accepted_go_proof_mismatch'
}
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $runtimeDir) { throw 'fresh_runtime_path_exists_refuse_adoption' }
if ((Get-Sha256 $sourceSupervisor) -cne $supervisorSha -or
    (Get-Sha256 $sourceCloudflared) -cne $cloudflaredSha -or
    (Get-Item -LiteralPath $sourceEmptyConfig).Length -ne 0) { throw 'accepted_source_binary_or_config_mismatch' }
$knownExecutablePaths = @($st06SupervisorPath, $st06CloudflaredPath, $sourceSupervisor, $sourceCloudflared, $supervisorPath, $cloudflaredPath)
foreach ($knownPath in $knownExecutablePaths) {
    if ((Get-ExactPathMatches -Path $knownPath).Count -gt 0) { throw 'known_owned_tunnel_process_present_refuse_duplicate_or_adoption' }
}

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $runtimeDir | Out-Null
Copy-Item -LiteralPath $sourceSupervisor -Destination $supervisorPath
Copy-Item -LiteralPath $sourceCloudflared -Destination $cloudflaredPath
$empty = [System.IO.File]::Open($emptyConfigPath, [System.IO.FileMode]::CreateNew, [System.IO.FileAccess]::Write, [System.IO.FileShare]::Read)
$empty.Flush($true)
$empty.Dispose()
if ((Get-Sha256 $supervisorPath) -cne $supervisorSha -or
    (Get-Sha256 $cloudflaredPath) -cne $cloudflaredSha -or
    (Get-Item -LiteralPath $emptyConfigPath).Length -ne 0 -or
    (Test-Path -LiteralPath $statePath)) { throw 'new_owned_runtime_prelaunch_guard_failed' }
foreach ($knownPath in $knownExecutablePaths) {
    if ((Get-ExactPathMatches -Path $knownPath).Count -gt 0) { throw 'known_owned_tunnel_process_appeared_before_launch' }
}

$startup = ([wmiclass]'Win32_ProcessStartup').CreateInstance()
$startup.ShowWindow = 0
$creation = ([wmiclass]'Win32_Process').Create(('"' + $supervisorPath + '"'), $null, $startup)
if ($creation.ReturnValue -ne 0 -or -not $creation.ProcessId) { throw 'owned_supervisor_creation_failed' }
$receipt = [ordered]@{
    checkpoint = 'stcon05-r41-owned-quick-tunnel-launch'
    capturedAtUtc = [DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('o')
    result = 'new_owned_supervisor_created'
    supervisorPid = [int]$creation.ProcessId
    supervisorSha256 = $supervisorSha
    cloudflaredSha256 = $cloudflaredSha
    target = $target
    newRuntimeDirectory = $runtimeDir
    oldProcessesStoppedOrAdopted = $false
    nextGate = 'read fresh runtime state and exact new process identity, then verify new hostname over normal TLS before any origin edit'
}
$receipt | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $launchReceiptPath -Encoding UTF8
$receipt | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress

```
